`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/B2BInquiryModal.tsx`:
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Product } from '../types';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';

interface B2BInquiryModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  product?: Product;
  selectedPackageId?: string;
  defaultChannel?: 'export' | 'wholesale' | 'general';
}

export const B2BInquiryModal: React.FC<B2BInquiryModalProps> = ({
  open,
  onClose,
  product,
  selectedPackageId,
  defaultChannel = 'wholesale',
}) => {
  const { language, localize } = useLanguage();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(open);
  const [closing, setClosing] = useState(false);

  const [company, setCompany] = useState('');
  const [contactName, setContactName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [country, setCountry] = useState('');
  const [volume, setVolume] = useState('');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  const copy = {
    ru: {
      defaultCountry: 'Беларусь',
        defaultVolume: '1–5 тонн / партия',
          prefix: 'Запрос спецификации и коммерческого предложения:',
          err: 'Укажите название компании, контактное лицо и корректный e-mail.',
          titleExport: 'Запрос в отдел экспорта',
          titleSales: 'Связаться с отделом продаж B2B',
          sentTitle: 'Заявка зарегистрирована',
          sentDesc: (c: string, e: string) =>
          `Специалист отдела продаж свяжется с ${c} (${e}) в течение 1 рабочего дня.`,
          close: 'Закрыть',
          company: 'Компания *',
          companyPlaceholder: 'ООО «Партнёр Трейд»',
          contact: 'Контактное лицо *',
          phone: 'Телефон',
          country: 'Страна поставки',
          volume: 'Плановый объём',
          message: 'Комментарий к запросу',
          cancel: 'Отмена',
          submit: 'Отправить запрос',
    },
    be: {
      defaultCountry: 'Беларусь',
        defaultVolume: '1–5 тон / партыя',
          prefix: 'Запыт спецыфікацыі і камерцыйнай прапановы:',
          err: 'Укажыце назву кампаніі, кантактную асобу і карэктны e-mail.',
          titleExport: 'Запыт у аддзел экспарту',
          titleSales: 'Звязацца з аддзелам продажаў B2B',
          sentTitle: 'Заяўка зарэгістравана',
          sentDesc: (c: string, e: string) =>
          `Спецыяліст аддзела продажаў звяжацца з ${c} (${e}) на працягу 1 працоўнага дня.`,
          close: 'Закрыць',
          company: 'Кампанія *',
          companyPlaceholder: 'ТАА «Партнёр Трэйд»',
          contact: 'Кантактная асоба *',
          phone: 'Тэлефон',
          country: 'Краіна пастаўкі',
          volume: 'Планавы аб’ём',
          message: 'Каментарый да запыту',
          cancel: 'Адмена',
          submit: 'Адправіць запыт',
    },
    en: {
      defaultCountry: 'Belarus / Export',
        defaultVolume: '1–5 tons / shipment',
          prefix: 'Commercial quote & spec request:',
          err: 'Please provide company name, contact person, and a valid email.',
          titleExport: 'Export Department Inquiry',
          titleSales: 'B2B Sales Department Inquiry',
          sentTitle: 'Inquiry Submitted',
          sentDesc: (c: string, e: string) =>
          `Our sales specialist will reply to ${c} (${e}) within 1 business day.`,
          close: 'Close',
          company: 'Company *',
          companyPlaceholder: 'Partner Trade LLC',
          contact: 'Contact Person *',
          phone: 'Phone',
          country: 'Destination Country',
          volume: 'Estimated Volume',
          message: 'Message',
          cancel: 'Cancel',
          submit: 'Submit Inquiry',
    },
    zh: {
      defaultCountry: '中国 / 出口目的地',
        defaultVolume: '1–5 吨 / 批次',
          prefix: '产品规格与商业报价咨询:',
          err: '请填写公司名称、联系人及有效的电子邮箱。',
          titleExport: '国际出口部门咨询',
          titleSales: '联系 B2B 销售部门',
          sentTitle: '您的询盘已成功提交',
          sentDesc: (c: string, e: string) =>
          `我们的外贸及销售专员将在 1 个工作日内联系 ${c} (${e})。`,
          close: '关闭',
          company: '公司名称 *',
          companyPlaceholder: '某某国际贸易有限公司',
          contact: '联系人 *',
          phone: '联系电话',
          country: '目的国/地区',
          volume: '预计采购量',
          message: '咨询详情',
          cancel: '取消',
          submit: '提交咨询',
    },
  }[language];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setMounted(true);
      setClosing(false);
      return;
    }

    if (mounted) {
      setClosing(true);
      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        setMounted(false);
        setClosing(false);
      }, 180);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [open, mounted]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setSubmitted(false);
    setError('');
    setCountry((prev) => prev || copy.defaultCountry);
    setVolume((prev) => prev || copy.defaultVolume);

    if (product) {
      const pkg = product.packages.find((p) => p.id === selectedPackageId) || product.packages[0];
      setMessage(`${copy.prefix} ${localize(product.name)} (${pkg?.weight || ''})`);
    } else {
      setMessage('');
    }
  }, [open, product, selectedPackageId, language, localize, copy.defaultCountry, copy.defaultVolume, copy.prefix]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
      window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!mounted) return null;

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!company.trim() || !contactName.trim() || !email.includes('@')) {
      setError(copy.err);
      return;
    }
    setError('');
    setSubmitted(true);
  };

  return (
    <div
    className={`fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-slate-900/55 backdrop-blur-xs ${
      closing ? 'modal-backdrop-out pointer-events-none' : 'modal-backdrop-in'
    }`}
    role="dialog"
    aria-modal="true"
    onClick={(e) => {
      if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
    }}
    >
    <div
    className={`bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-lg max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto shadow-2xl ${
      closing ? 'modal-panel-out' : 'modal-panel-in'
    }`}
    >
    <div className="px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
    <div>
    <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
    {defaultChannel === 'export' ? copy.titleExport : copy.titleSales}
    </h2>
    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">ved@milk.by · sales@milk.by</p>
    </div>
    <button
    type="button"
    onClick={onClose}
    aria-label="Close modal"
    className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors"
    >
    <X className="w-4 h-4" />
    </button>
    </div>

    <div className="p-5 sm:p-6">
    {submitted ? (
      <div className="py-6 text-center space-y-4">
      <CheckCircle2 className="w-12 h-12 text-[#0057B8] mx-auto" strokeWidth={1.5} />
      <div className="space-y-1">
      <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
      {copy.sentTitle}
      </h3>
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">{copy.sentDesc(company, email)}</p>
      </div>
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClose}
      className="px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors"
      >
      {copy.close}
      </button>
      </div>
    ) : (
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 text-xs font-medium text-red-700 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg">
        {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
      {copy.company}
      </label>
      <input
      type="text"
      required
      value={company}
      onChange={(e) => setCompany(e.target.value)}
      placeholder={copy.companyPlaceholder}
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>
      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
      {copy.contact}
      </label>
      <input
      type="text"
      required
      value={contactName}
      onChange={(e) => setContactName(e.target.value)}
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">E-mail *</label>
      <input
      type="email"
      required
      value={email}
      onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
      placeholder="buyer@company.com"
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>
      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
      {copy.phone}
      </label>
      <input
      type="tel"
      value={phone}
      onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
      placeholder="+375 ..."
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
      {copy.country}
      </label>
      <input
      type="text"
      value={country}
      onChange={(e) => setCountry(e.target.value)}
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>
      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
      {copy.volume}
      </label>
      <input
      type="text"
      value={volume}
      onChange={(e) => setVolume(e.target.value)}
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>
      </div>

      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
      {copy.message}
      </label>
      <textarea
      rows={3}
      value={message}
      onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
      className="w-full p-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>

      <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClose}
      className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
      >
      {copy.cancel}
      </button>
      <button
      type="submit"
      className="px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors"
      >
      {copy.submit}
      </button>
      </div>
      </form>
    )}
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

```
import React from 'react';

interface CategoryIconProps {
    categoryId: string;
    className?: string;
}

export const CategoryIcon: React.FC<CategoryIconProps> = ({
    categoryId,
    className = 'w-9 h-9',
}) => {
    switch (categoryId) {
        case 'all':
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect x="8" y="8" width="13.5" height="13.5" rx="4" fill="currentColor" />
                <rect
                x="26.5"
                y="8"
                width="13.5"
                height="13.5"
                rx="4"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.4"
                />
                <rect x="8" y="26.5" width="13.5" height="13.5" rx="4" fill="currentColor" />
                <rect x="26.5" y="26.5" width="13.5" height="13.5" rx="4" fill="currentColor" />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'milk':
            // Bottle silhouette with curved milk wave (inspired by milk.by)
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect x="19" y="6" width="10" height="3.5" rx="1.75" fill="currentColor" />
                <path
                d="M20 9.5H28V13.2C28 15.6 33 17.6 33 22.8V24.6C29.5 22.8 26.8 22.8 24 24.6C21.2 26.4 18.5 26.4 15 24.6V22.8C15 17.6 20 15.6 20 13.2V9.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.28"
                />
                <path

                fill="currentColor"

                d="M31 30.9C36.3 29.7 40.4 26.9 41 23.6V31.8C40.4 35.1 36.3 37.9 31 39.1V30.9Z"

                d="M24.2 29L37.4 21.5V27.3L24.2 34.8V29Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.78"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'desserts':
            // Dessert cup with diagonal spoon (inspired by milk.by)
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <path
                d="M29.4 17.5L34.5 9.4C35.1 8.4 36.4 8.1 37.3 8.7C38.2 9.3 38.5 10.6 37.9 11.5L34 17.8L29.4 17.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                />
                <rect x="12.5" y="17" width="23" height="4" rx="2" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity="0.45" />
                <path
                d="M14.5 21H33.5L31.2 37.6C30.9 39.6 29.2 41 27.2 41H20.8C18.8 41 17.1 39.6 16.8 37.6L14.5 21Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'yogurt':
            // Greek yogurt cup with creamy dome swirl & spoon
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <path
                d="M29 15.5L33.8 8.8C34.4 7.9 35.6 7.7 36.5 8.3C37.4 8.9 37.6 10.1 37 11L33 16.5L29 15.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.65"

                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.4"
                />
                <rect x="12" y="18" width="24" height="3.5" rx="1.75" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity="0.6" />
                <path
                d="M14 21.5H34L31.7 37.6C31.4 39.6 29.7 41 27.7 41H20.3C18.3 41 16.6 39.6 16.3 37.6L14 21.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'fermented':
            // Kefir bottle with probiotic bubbles
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect x="18.5" y="6" width="11" height="3.5" rx="1.75" fill="currentColor" />
                <path
                d="M19.5 9.5H28.5V13.5C28.5 15.8 33.5 17.8 33.5 23V37.5C33.5 39.985 31.485 42 29 42H19C16.515 42 14.5 39.985 14.5 37.5V23C14.5 17.8 19.5 15.8 19.5 13.5V9.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"

                </svg>
            );

        case 'sour-cream':
            // Wide sour cream pot with lid & spoon
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <path
                d="M28 16L33.5 9.5C34.2 8.7 35.4 8.6 36.2 9.3C37 10 37.1 11.2 36.4 12L32.5 16.5L28 16Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.65"
                />
                <rect x="9.5" y="16" width="29" height="4" rx="2" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity="0.45" />
                <path

                fill="currentColor"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'tvorog':
            // Bowl with crumbly cottage cheese / tvorog curds
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <circle cx="17.5" cy="20" r="4" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity="0.48" />
                <circle cx="24" cy="17.5" r="4.5" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity="0.65" />
                <circle cx="30.5" cy="20" r="4" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity="0.48" />
                <path
                d="M9 23.5H39C39 32.889 32.284 40.5 24 40.5C15.716 40.5 9 32.889 9 23.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'ice-cream':
            // Classic rounded popsicle on stick (inspired by milk.by)
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect x="15.5" y="7" width="17" height="25" rx="8.5" fill="currentColor" />
                <path
                d="M15.5 23.5C18.5 25.2 21 25.2 24 23.5C27 21.8 29.5 21.8 32.5 23.5C32.5 28.2 28.7 32 24 32C19.3 32 15.5 28.2 15.5 23.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.65"
                />
                <rect
                x="21.5"
                y="32"
                width="5"
                height="10"
                rx="2.5"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.52"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'cream':
            // Aseptic Tetra Pak / Gable carton with droplet
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <path d="M15 16.5L24 9L33 16.5H15Z" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity="0.45" />
                <rect x="19" y="6.5" width="10" height="2.8" rx="1.4" fill="currentColor" />
                <path
                d="M15 16.5H33V38.5C33 39.881 31.881 41 30.5 41H17.5C16.119 41 15 39.881 15 38.5V16.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                />
                <path
                d="M24 22.5C24 22.5 28 27.3 28 30.1C28 32.3 26.2 34.1 24 34.1C21.8 34.1 20 32.3 20 30.1C20 27.3 24 22.5 24 22.5Z"
                fill="white"
                fillOpacity="0.4"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'dry-dairy':
            // Gusseted kraft bag with folded seal (inspired by milk.by)
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect x="14.5" y="7.5" width="19" height="4.5" rx="1.5" fill="currentColor" />
                <path
                d="M15 13.5H33L36.5 39.5C36.6 40.3 36 41 35.2 41H12.8C12 41 11.4 40.3 11.5 39.5L15 13.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.88"
                />
                <path d="M16 41L19.5 32.5L23 41H16Z" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity="0.45" />
                <path d="M25 41L28.5 32.5L32 41H25Z" fill="currentColor" fillOpacity="0.45" />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'other':
            // Drinking glass with water/whey droplet inside (inspired by milk.by)
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <path
                d="M12.5 10.5H35.5L32.1 38.4C31.9 40.2 30.4 41.5 28.6 41.5H19.4C17.6 41.5 16.1 40.2 15.9 38.4L12.5 10.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.22"
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth="2.8"
                strokeLinejoin="round"
                />
                <path
                d="M24 18.5C24 18.5 29.2 24.7 29.2 28.4C29.2 31.3 26.9 33.6 24 33.6C21.1 33.6 18.8 31.3 18.8 28.4C18.8 24.7 24 18.5 24 18.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        default:
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <path
                d="M24 9C24 9 35 21.8 35 29.5C35 35.8 30.1 40.5 24 40.5C17.9 40.5 13 35.8 13 29.5C13 21.8 24 9 24 9Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.88"
                />
                </svg>
            );
    }
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/Filters.tsx`:
```
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { RotateCcw, SlidersHorizontal, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { Category, Product } from '../types';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';

export interface CatalogFilterState {
  categoryId: string;
  subcategoryId: string;
  fatRange: 'all' | 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';
  weightRange: 'all' | 'retail' | 'medium' | 'bulk';
  shelfLifeRange: 'all' | 'fresh' | 'medium' | 'long';
  proteinMin: 'all' | '8' | '15' | '24';
  lactoseFreeOnly: boolean;
  highProteinOnly: boolean;
  exportReadyOnly: boolean;
  characteristics: Record<string, string>;
  sortBy: 'featured' | 'fat-asc' | 'fat-desc' | 'protein-desc' | 'name';
}

interface FiltersProps {
  categories: Category[];
  products: Product[];
  state: CatalogFilterState;
  onChange: (next: CatalogFilterState) => void;
  onReset: () => void;
  totalCount: number;
}

export const Filters: React.FC<FiltersProps> = ({
  categories,
  products,
  state,
  onChange,
  onReset,
  totalCount,
}) => {
  const { language, localize } = useLanguage();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const activeCategory = categories.find((c) => c.id === state.categoryId);

  const labels = {
    ru: {

    },
    en: {
      filtersToggle: 'Filters & Sorting',

      sortFatDesc: 'Fat: High to Low',

      found: 'Items:',
      reset: 'Reset',
    },
    zh: {
      filtersToggle: '筛选与排序',
      category: '产品分类',
      allCategories: '全部分类',
      subcategory: '细分系列',
      allSubs: '全部子项',
      fat: '乳脂含量',
      fatAll: '不限',
      fatLow: '≤ 5% 低脂',
      fatMed: '15% – 35%',
      fatHigh: '≥ 45% 奶酪黄油',
      weight: '包装规格',
      weightAll: '不限规格',
      weightRetail: '≤ 250克（零售小包装）',
      weightMedium: '251克 – 1公斤',
      weightBulk: '> 1公斤（餐饮 / B2B大包装）',
      shelfLife: '保质期',
      shelfAll: '不限',
      shelfFresh: '≤ 21天（短保鲜品）',
      shelfMed: '22 – 90天',
      shelfLong: '> 90天（常温 / 出口长保）',
      protein: '蛋白质门槛',
      proteinAll: '不限',
      protein8: '≥ 8克 / 100克',
      protein15: '≥ 15克 / 100克',
      protein24: '≥ 24克 / 100克',
      lactoseFree: '无乳糖',
      highProtein: '高蛋白 (≥8克)',
      exportReady: '支持出口 / B2B',
      sort: '排序',
      sortFeatured: '默认推荐',
      sortFatAsc: '脂肪含量 ↑',
      sortFatDesc: '脂肪含量 ↓',
      sortProtein: '蛋白质从高到低',
      sortName: '按名称',
      anyOption: '不限',
      found: '产品数:',
      reset: '重置',
    },
  }[language];

  const dynamicCharacteristicFilters = useMemo(() => {
    if (!activeCategory) return [];
    const catProducts = products.filter(
      (p) => p.visible && p.category === activeCategory.id
    );
    return activeCategory.characteristicSchema
    .map((field) => {
      const optionSet = new Set<string>();
      for (const p of catProducts) {
        const val = localize(p.characteristics[field.key]);
        if (val) optionSet.add(val);
      }
      return {
        key: field.key,
        label: localize(field.label),
         options: Array.from(optionSet),
      };
    })
    .filter((f) => f.options.length > 0);
  }, [activeCategory, products, localize]);

  const showLactoseFilter = !activeCategory || activeCategory.filterKeys.includes('lactoseFree');
  const showProteinFilter = !activeCategory || activeCategory.filterKeys.includes('highProtein');
  const showExportFilter = !activeCategory || activeCategory.filterKeys.includes('exportReady');

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
    <div className="flex sm:hidden items-center justify-between gap-3">
    <button
    type="button"
    onClick={() => setMobileOpen((prev) => !prev)}
    aria-expanded={mobileOpen}
    className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800"
    >
    <SlidersHorizontal className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8]" />
    <span>{labels.filtersToggle}</span>
    <ChevronDown
    className={`w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 transition-transform ${mobileOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
    />
    </button>
    <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
    {labels.found} <strong className="text-slate-900">{totalCount}</strong>
    </span>
    </div>

    <div className={`${mobileOpen ? 'block' : 'hidden'} sm:block space-y-4`}>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
    <div>
    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1.5">
    {labels.category}
    </label>
    <select
    value={state.categoryId}
    onChange={(e) =>
      onChange({
        ...state,
        categoryId: e.target.value,
        subcategoryId: 'all',
        characteristics: {},
      })
    }
    className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
    >
    <option value="all">{labels.allCategories}</option>
    {categories.map((cat) => (
      <option key={cat.id} value={cat.id}>
      {localize(cat.name)}
      </option>
    ))}
    </select>
    </div>

    <div>
    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1.5">
    {labels.subcategory}
    </label>
    <select
    value={state.subcategoryId}
    disabled={!activeCategory || activeCategory.subcategories.length === 0}
    onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...state, subcategoryId: e.target.value })}
    className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-slate-900 disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
    >
    <option value="all">{labels.allSubs}</option>
    {activeCategory?.subcategories.map((sub) => (
      <option key={sub.id} value={sub.id}>
      {localize(sub.name)}
      </option>
    ))}
    </select>
    </div>

    <div>
    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1.5">
    {labels.fat}
    </label>
    <select
    value={state.fatRange}
    onChange={(e) =>
      onChange({ ...state, fatRange: e.target.value as CatalogFilterState['fatRange'] })
    }
    className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
    >
    <option value="all">{labels.fatAll}</option>
    <option value="low">{labels.fatLow}</option>
    <option value="medium">{labels.fatMed}</option>
    <option value="high">{labels.fatHigh}</option>
    </select>
    </div>

    <div>
    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1.5">
    {labels.sort}
    </label>
    <select
    value={state.sortBy}
    onChange={(e) =>
      onChange({ ...state, sortBy: e.target.value as CatalogFilterState['sortBy'] })
    }
    className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
    >
    <option value="featured">{labels.sortFeatured}</option>
    <option value="fat-asc">{labels.sortFatAsc}</option>
    <option value="fat-desc">{labels.sortFatDesc}</option>
    <option value="protein-desc">{labels.sortProtein}</option>
    <option value="name">{labels.sortName}</option>
    </select>
    </div>

    <div>
    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1.5">
    {labels.weight}
    </label>
    <select
    value={state.weightRange}
    onChange={(e) =>
      onChange({
        ...state,
        weightRange: e.target.value as CatalogFilterState['weightRange'],
      })
    }
    className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
    >
    <option value="all">{labels.weightAll}</option>
    <option value="retail">{labels.weightRetail}</option>
    <option value="medium">{labels.weightMedium}</option>
    <option value="bulk">{labels.weightBulk}</option>
    </select>
    </div>

    <div>
    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1.5">
    {labels.shelfLife}
    </label>
    <select
    value={state.shelfLifeRange}
    onChange={(e) =>
      onChange({
        ...state,
        shelfLifeRange: e.target.value as CatalogFilterState['shelfLifeRange'],
      })
    }
    className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
    >
    <option value="all">{labels.shelfAll}</option>
    <option value="fresh">{labels.shelfFresh}</option>
    <option value="medium">{labels.shelfMed}</option>
    <option value="long">{labels.shelfLong}</option>
    </select>
    </div>

    <div>
    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1.5">
    {labels.protein}
    </label>
    <select
    value={state.proteinMin}
    onChange={(e) =>
      onChange({
        ...state,
        proteinMin: e.target.value as CatalogFilterState['proteinMin'],
      })
    }
    className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
    >
    <option value="all">{labels.proteinAll}</option>
    <option value="8">{labels.protein8}</option>
    <option value="15">{labels.protein15}</option>
    <option value="24">{labels.protein24}</option>
    </select>
    </div>
    </div>

    {/* Dynamic category-specific characteristic filters */}
    {dynamicCharacteristicFilters.length > 0 && (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200/70">
      {dynamicCharacteristicFilters.map((charField) => (
        <div key={charField.key}>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1.5">
        {charField.label}
        </label>
        <select
        value={state.characteristics[charField.key] || 'all'}
        onChange={(e) =>
          onChange({
            ...state,
            characteristics: {
              ...state.characteristics,
              [charField.key]: e.target.value,
            },
          })
        }
        className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
        >
        <option value="all">{labels.anyOption}</option>
        {charField.options.map((opt) => (
          <option key={opt} value={opt}>
          {opt}
          </option>
        ))}
        </select>
        </div>
      ))}
      </div>
    )}

    <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-200/80 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-sm text-slate-700">
    {showLactoseFilter && (
      <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
      <input
      type="checkbox"
      checked={state.lactoseFreeOnly}
      onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...state, lactoseFreeOnly: e.target.checked })}
      className="w-4 h-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-[#0057B8] focus:ring-[#0057B8]"
      />
      <span>{labels.lactoseFree}</span>
      </label>
    )}

    {showProteinFilter && (
      <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
      <input
      type="checkbox"
      checked={state.highProteinOnly}
      onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...state, highProteinOnly: e.target.checked })}
      className="w-4 h-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-[#0057B8] focus:ring-[#0057B8]"
      />
      <span>{labels.highProtein}</span>
      </label>
    )}

    {showExportFilter && (
      <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
      <input
      type="checkbox"
      checked={state.exportReadyOnly}
      onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...state, exportReadyOnly: e.target.checked })}
      className="w-4 h-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-[#0057B8] focus:ring-[#0057B8]"
      />
      <span>{labels.exportReady}</span>
      </label>
    )}
    </div>

    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
    <span className="hidden sm:inline">
    {labels.found} <strong className="text-slate-900">{totalCount}</strong>
    </span>
    <button
    type="button"
    onClick={onReset}
    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0057B8] font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
    >
    <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
    {labels.reset}
    </button>
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/Footer.tsx`:

```
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';

export const Footer: React.FC = () => {
  const { t, language } = useLanguage();

  const labels = {

    {/* Компактная декоративная волна под нижним текстом */}

    className="pointer-events-none select-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-8 sm:h-10"
    aria-hidden="true"
    >
    <svg
    viewBox="0 0 1440 60"
    preserveAspectRatio="none"
    className="block w-full h-full"
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/Header.tsx`:
```
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Menu, X, ChevronDown, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { LanguageSwitcher } from './LanguageSwitcher';
import logoUrl from '../assets/logo.webp';

export const Header: React.FC = () => {
  const { t, localize, language } = useLanguage();
  const { categories } = useCatalogStore();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [openMenu, setOpenMenu] = useState<'catalog' | 'business' | null>(null);
  const [pastHero, setPastHero] = useState(false);

  const isHomePage =
  location.pathname === '/' || /^\/(ru|be|en|zh)\/?$/.test(location.pathname);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => {
      setPastHero(window.scrollY > 440);
    };
    handleScroll();
    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
  }, [location.pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMobileOpen(false);
    setOpenMenu(null);
  }, [location.pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mobileOpen) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setMobileOpen(false);
    };
      window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [mobileOpen]);

  const isOverHero = isHomePage && !pastHero;

  const isActive = (path: string) =>
  location.pathname === path || location.pathname.startsWith(`${path}/`);

  const isBusinessActive = isActive('/partners') || isActive('/export');

  const copy = {
    ru: {
      allCatalog: 'Весь каталог продукции',
      wholesale: 'Оптовым покупателям и HoReCa',
      wholesaleSub: 'Поставки для сетей, пиццерий и пекарен',
      exportTitle: 'Международный экспорт',
      exportSub: 'СОМ, масло, сыры и сертификаты GACC / ISO',
    },
    be: {
      allCatalog: 'Увесь каталог прадукцыі',
      wholesale: 'Аптовым пакупнікам і HoReCa',
      wholesaleSub: 'Пастаўкі для сетак, піцэрый і пякарняў',
      exportTitle: 'Міжнародны экспарт',
      exportSub: 'САМ, масла, сыры і сертыфікаты GACC / ISO',
    },
    en: {
      allCatalog: 'View All Products',
      wholesale: 'Wholesale & HoReCa Partners',
      wholesaleSub: 'Supply for retail chains, pizzerias, and bakeries',
      exportTitle: 'Global Export Portal',
      exportSub: 'SMP, butter, aged cheese, GACC & ISO docs',
    },
    zh: {
      allCatalog: '浏览全部产品目录',
      wholesale: '大宗批发与 HoReCa 餐饮合作',
      wholesaleSub: '面向连锁商超、披萨门店与工业烘焙直供',
      exportTitle: '国际出口专区',
      exportSub: '脱脂奶粉、黄油、奶酪及 GACC / ISO 认证',
    },
  }[language];

  const linkClass = (active: boolean) =>
  `nav-underline-link inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 py-2 transition-colors duration-200 ${
    isOverHero
    ? active
    ? 'text-white'
    : 'text-white/90 hover:text-white'
    : active
    ? 'text-[#0057B8]'
    : 'text-slate-700 hover:text-[#0057B8]'
  }`;

  return (
    <header
    className={`${
      isHomePage ? 'fixed top-0 left-0 right-0' : 'sticky top-0'
    } z-40 transition-colors duration-200 backdrop-blur-md ${
      isOverHero
      ? mobileOpen
      ? 'bg-slate-950/60 border-b border-white/15'
      : 'bg-black/25 border-b border-white/15'
      : mobileOpen
      ? 'bg-white/80 border-b border-slate-200/80'
      : 'bg-white/90 border-b border-slate-200/80'
    }`}
    >
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 h-[72px] flex items-center justify-between gap-6">
    {/* Zone 1: Brand Logo */}
    <Link
    to="/"
    onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
    aria-label={t('brandName')}
    className="inline-flex items-center shrink-0 rounded-lg transition-transform duration-200 hover:scale-[1.02] focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-[#0057B8]"
    >
    <img
    src={logoUrl}
    alt={t('brandName')}
    className="h-11 sm:h-12 w-auto object-contain"
    />
    </Link>

    {/* Zone 2: Clean 5-Item Navigation with Dropdowns & Animated Underlines */}
    <nav
    className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-7 text-[15px] font-medium"
    aria-label="Main navigation"
    >
    {/* 1. Products + Category Dropdown */}
    <div
    className="relative group/nav"
    onMouseEnter={() => setOpenMenu('catalog')}
    onMouseLeave={() => setOpenMenu(null)}
    >
    <Link
    to="/catalog"
    data-active={isActive('/catalog')}
    className={linkClass(isActive('/catalog'))}
    >
    <span>{t('navCatalog')}</span>
    <ChevronDown
    className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 ${
      openMenu === 'catalog' ? 'rotate-180' : ''
    }`}
    />
    </Link>

    {openMenu === 'catalog' && (
      <div className="absolute left-0 top-full pt-2 z-50">
      <div className="w-[460px] rounded-2xl bg-white/98 backdrop-blur-xl border border-slate-200/90 shadow-xl p-3 space-y-2">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1">
      {categories.map((cat) => (
        <Link
        key={cat.id}
        to={`/catalog/${cat.slug}`}
        onClick={() => setOpenMenu(null)}
        className="group/item px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-700 hover:bg-blue-50/70 hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
        >
        <span className="truncate">{localize(cat.name)}</span>
        <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 opacity-0 -translate-x-1 group-hover/item:opacity-100 group-hover/item:translate-x-0 transition-all duration-200 shrink-0" />
        </Link>
      ))}
      </div>
      <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
      <Link
      to="/catalog"
      onClick={() => setOpenMenu(null)}
      className="group/all flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-[#0057B8] hover:bg-blue-50 transition-colors"
      >
      <span>{copy.allCatalog}</span>
      <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover/all:translate-x-0.5" />
      </Link>
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>
    )}
    </div>

    {/* 2. B2B & Export Dropdown */}
    <div
    className="relative group/nav"
    onMouseEnter={() => setOpenMenu('business')}
    onMouseLeave={() => setOpenMenu(null)}
    >
    <Link
    to="/export"
    data-active={isBusinessActive}
    className={linkClass(isBusinessActive)}
    >
    <span>{t('navBusiness')}</span>
    <ChevronDown
    className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 ${
      openMenu === 'business' ? 'rotate-180' : ''
    }`}
    />
    </Link>

    {openMenu === 'business' && (
      <div className="absolute left-0 top-full pt-2 z-50">
      <div className="w-80 rounded-2xl bg-white/98 backdrop-blur-xl border border-slate-200/90 shadow-xl p-2 space-y-1">
      <Link
      to="/partners"
      onClick={() => setOpenMenu(null)}
      className="block px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-blue-50/70 transition-colors group/item"
      >
      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 group-hover/item:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
      {copy.wholesale}
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{copy.wholesaleSub}</div>
      </Link>
      <Link
      to="/export"
      onClick={() => setOpenMenu(null)}
      className="block px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-blue-50/70 transition-colors group/item"
      >
      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 group-hover/item:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
      {copy.exportTitle}
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{copy.exportSub}</div>
      </Link>
      </div>
      </div>
    )}
    </div>

    {/* 3. News */}
    <Link to="/news" data-active={isActive('/news')} className={linkClass(isActive('/news'))}>
    {t('navNews')}
    </Link>

    {/* 4. About */}
    <Link to="/about" data-active={isActive('/about')} className={linkClass(isActive('/about'))}>
    {t('navAbout')}
    </Link>

    {/* 5. Contacts */}
    <Link
    to="/contacts"
    data-active={isActive('/contacts')}
    className={linkClass(isActive('/contacts'))}
    >
    {t('navContacts')}
    </Link>
    </nav>

    {/* Zone 3: Language Switcher + Mobile Toggle */}
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
    <div className="hidden sm:inline-flex">
    <LanguageSwitcher variant="dropdown" dark={isOverHero} />
    </div>

    <button
    type="button"
    onClick={() => setMobileOpen((prev) => !prev)}
    className={`lg:hidden inline-flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-lg border transition-colors ${
      isOverHero
      ? 'border-white/25 text-white hover:bg-white/10'
      : 'border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
    }`}
    aria-expanded={mobileOpen}
    aria-label="Toggle navigation menu"
    >
    {mobileOpen ? <X className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />}
    </button>
    </div>
    </div>

    {/* Mobile Drawer */}
    {mobileOpen && (
      <div
      className={`lg:hidden border-t max-h-[calc(100dvh-72px)] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain px-4 py-4 space-y-4 ${
        isOverHero
        ? 'border-white/15 text-white'
        : 'border-slate-200/70 text-slate-900'
      }`}
      >
      <nav className="flex flex-col space-y-1" aria-label="Mobile navigation">
      {[
        { to: '/catalog', label: t('navCatalog') },
                    { to: '/partners', label: t('navPartners') },
                    { to: '/export', label: t('navExport') },
                    { to: '/news', label: t('navNews') },
                    { to: '/about', label: t('navAbout') },
                    { to: '/contacts', label: t('navContacts') },
      ].map((item) => (
        <Link
        key={item.to}
        to={item.to}
        onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
        className={`px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-base font-medium transition-colors ${
          isOverHero
          ? isActive(item.to)
          ? 'bg-white/15 text-white'
          : 'text-white/90 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white'
      : isActive(item.to)
      ? 'bg-[#0057B8]/10 text-[#0057B8]'
      : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-900/5 hover:text-[#0057B8]'
        }`}
        >
        {item.label}
        </Link>
      ))}
      </nav>

      <div
      className={`pt-3 border-t flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ${
        isOverHero ? 'border-white/15' : 'border-slate-200/70'
      }`}
      >
      <span className={`text-xs ${isOverHero ? 'text-white/75' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>
      Language / Язык
      </span>
      <LanguageSwitcher compact dark={isOverHero} />
      </div>
      </div>
    )}
    </header>
  );
};
```

```
  initials: string;
  accentClass: string;
  channel: 'export' | 'wholesale' | 'general';
  email: string;

  name: Record<'ru' | 'be' | 'en' | 'zh', string>;
  role: Record<'ru' | 'be' | 'en' | 'zh', string>;
  bio: Record<'ru' | 'be' | 'en' | 'zh', string>;
}

function getCardsPerView(): number {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 3;
  if (window.innerWidth >= 1024) return 3;
  if (window.innerWidth >= 640) return 2;
  return 1;
}

export const HomeTeamSlider: React.FC<HomeTeamSliderProps> = ({ onRequestContact }) => {
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();
  const [cardsPerView, setCardsPerView] = useState<number>(getCardsPerView);
  const [slide, setSlide] = useState(0);
  const [dragOffset, setDragOffset] = useState(0);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);

  const viewportRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const slideRef = useRef(0);
  const maxSlideRef = useRef(Math.max(0, TEAM_PROFILES.length - cardsPerView));
  const cardsPerViewRef = useRef(cardsPerView);

  const maxSlide = Math.max(0, TEAM_PROFILES.length - cardsPerView);

  useEffect(() => {
    slideRef.current = slide;
  }, [slide]);

  useEffect(() => {
    cardsPerViewRef.current = cardsPerView;
    maxSlideRef.current = Math.max(0, TEAM_PROFILES.length - cardsPerView);
  }, [cardsPerView]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onResize = () => {
      const nextPerView = getCardsPerView();
      if (nextPerView === cardsPerViewRef.current) return;
      cardsPerViewRef.current = nextPerView;
      setCardsPerView(nextPerView);
      const nextMax = Math.max(0, TEAM_PROFILES.length - nextPerView);
      maxSlideRef.current = nextMax;
      setSlide((prev) => Math.min(prev, nextMax));
    };
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const viewport = viewportRef.current;
    if (!viewport) return;

    let startX = 0;
    let startY = 0;
    let startTime = 0;
    let lockAxis: 'x' | 'y' | null = null;
    let currentDx = 0;

    const onTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => {
      if (e.touches.length !== 1) return;
      startX = e.touches[0].clientX;
      startY = e.touches[0].clientY;
      startTime = Date.now();
      lockAxis = null;
      currentDx = 0;
    };

    const onTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => {
      if (e.touches.length !== 1) return;
      const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - startX;
      const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - startY;

      if (lockAxis === null) {
        if (Math.abs(dx) < 8 && Math.abs(dy) < 8) return;
        if (Math.abs(dy) >= Math.abs(dx)) {
          lockAxis = 'y';
          return;
        }
        lockAxis = 'x';
        setIsDragging(true);
      }

      if (lockAxis === 'x') {
        if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
        const atStart = slideRef.current === 0 && dx > 0;
        const atEnd = slideRef.current >= maxSlideRef.current && dx < 0;
        currentDx = atStart || atEnd ? dx * 0.25 : dx;
        setDragOffset(currentDx);
      }
    };

    const finishTouch = () => {
      if (lockAxis === 'x') {
        const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
        const threshold = elapsed < 220 ? 25 : 50;
        const cur = slideRef.current;
        const max = maxSlideRef.current;

        if (currentDx < -threshold && cur < max) {
          setSlide(cur + 1);
        } else if (currentDx > threshold && cur > 0) {
          setSlide(cur - 1);
        }
      }
      lockAxis = null;
      currentDx = 0;
      setIsDragging(false);
      setDragOffset(0);
    };

    viewport.addEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart, { passive: true });
    viewport.addEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove, { passive: false });
    viewport.addEventListener('touchend', finishTouch, { passive: true });
    viewport.addEventListener('touchcancel', finishTouch, { passive: true });

    return () => {
      viewport.removeEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart);
      viewport.removeEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove);
      viewport.removeEventListener('touchend', finishTouch);
      viewport.removeEventListener('touchcancel', finishTouch);
    };
  }, []);

  const goToSlide = (idx: number) => {
    setIsDragging(false);
    setDragOffset(0);
    setSlide(Math.max(0, Math.min(maxSlide, idx)));
  };

  const copy = {
    ru: {
      eyebrow: 'Экспертная команда',
      title: 'Ключевые специалисты B2B и экспорта',
      subtitle: 'Прямой контакт с руководителями направлений по вопросам оптовых поставок, сертификации и экспортной логистики.',
      prev: 'Предыдущие специалисты',
      next: 'Следующие специалисты',
      slideDot: (i: number) => `Перейти к слайду ${i + 1}`,
      inquiryBtn: 'Запросить КП',
    },
    be: {
      eyebrow: 'Экспертная каманда',
      title: 'Ключавыя спецыялісты B2B і экспарту',
      subtitle: 'Прамы кантакт з кіраўнікамі напрамкаў па пытаннях аптовых паставак, сертыфікацыі і экспартнай лагістыкі.',
      prev: 'Папярэднія спецыялісты',
      next: 'Наступныя спецыялісты',
      slideDot: (i: number) => `Перайсці да слайда ${i + 1}`,
      inquiryBtn: 'Запытаць КП',
    },
    en: {
      eyebrow: 'Our Specialists',
      title: 'Dedicated B2B, Quality & Export Team',
      subtitle: 'Connect directly with department leads for wholesale pricing, product specifications, and export documentation.',
      prev: 'Previous specialists',
      next: 'Next specialists',
      slideDot: (i: number) => `Go to slide ${i + 1}`,
      inquiryBtn: 'Send Inquiry',
    },
    zh: {
      eyebrow: '专家团队',
      title: 'B2B 销售、品控与出口核心团队',
      subtitle: '直接对接各业务部门负责人，快速获取大宗报价、产品技术规格书及国际出口物流方案。',
      prev: '上一组专家',
      next: '下一组专家',
      slideDot: (i: number) => `切换到第 ${i + 1} 组`,
      inquiryBtn: '发送询盘',
    },
  }[language];

  const dotCount = maxSlide + 1;

  return (
    <section id="team" className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-6 sm:space-y-8">
    <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-5 sm:gap-6">
    <div className="max-w-2xl space-y-3">
    <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#0057B8]">
    {copy.eyebrow}
    </p>
    <h2 className="font-display text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
    {copy.title}
    </h2>
    <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{copy.subtitle}</p>
    </div>

    <Link
    to="/contacts"
    className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 self-start md:self-auto"
    >
    <span>{t('ctaContact')}</span>
    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
    </Link>
    </div>

    {/* Transform Carousel Viewport */}
    <div ref={viewportRef} className="overflow-hidden touch-pan-y select-none">
    <div
    className="flex gap-6 will-change-transform motion-reduce:transition-none"
    style={{
      transform: `translate3d(calc(-${slide} * ((100% + 24px) / ${cardsPerView}) + ${dragOffset}px), 0, 0)`,
          transition: isDragging ? 'none' : 'transform 320ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1)',
    }}
    >
    {TEAM_PROFILES.map((member) => (
      <article
      key={member.id}
      className="w-full min-w-0 sm:w-[calc((100%-24px)/2)] lg:w-[calc((100%-48px)/3)] shrink-0 flex flex-col justify-between bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-5"
      >
      <div className="space-y-4">
      <div
      className={`w-14 h-14 sm:w-16 sm:h-16 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center font-display text-lg font-bold ${member.accentClass}`}
      >
      {member.initials}
      </div>

      <div>
      <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
      {member.name[language]}
      </h3>
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-[#0057B8] mt-0.5">
      {member.role[language]}
      </p>
      </div>

      <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
      {member.bio[language]}
      </p>
      </div>

      <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <a
      href={`mailto:${member.email}`}
      title={member.email}
      aria-label={`${member.name[language]} e-mail`}
      className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0057B8] hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
      >
      <Mail className="w-4 h-4" />
      </a>
      <a
      href={`tel:${member.phone.replace(/[^+\d]/g, '')}`}
      title={member.phone}
      aria-label={`${member.name[language]} phone`}
      className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0057B8] hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
      >
      <Phone className="w-4 h-4" />
      </a>
      <Link
      to="/contacts"
      aria-label="Contacts"
      className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0057B8] hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
      >
      <Building2 className="w-4 h-4" />
      </Link>
      </div>

      {onRequestContact && (
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onRequestContact(member.channel)}
        className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0057B8] hover:text-[#004494] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
        >
        {copy.inquiryBtn}
        </button>
      )}
      </div>
      </article>
    ))}
    </div>
    </div>

    {/* Controls */}
    <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2" role="tablist" aria-label="Team carousel slides">
    {Array.from({ length: dotCount }).map((_, idx) => {
      const active = slide === idx;
      return (
        <button
        key={idx}
        type="button"
        aria-label={copy.slideDot(idx)}
        aria-pressed={active}
        onClick={() => goToSlide(idx)}
        className={`h-2.5 rounded-full transition-all ${
          active ? 'w-7 bg-[#0057B8]' : 'w-2.5 bg-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-400'
        }`}
        />
      );
    })}
    </div>

    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
    <button
    type="button"
    aria-label={copy.prev}
    onClick={() => goToSlide(slide - 1)}
    disabled={slide === 0}
    className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-11 sm:h-11 rounded-full border border-slate-300 bg-white text-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center hover:border-[#0057B8] hover:text-[#0057B8] disabled:opacity-35 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-colors"
    >
    <ChevronLeft className="w-5 h-5" />
    </button>
    <button
    type="button"
    aria-label={copy.next}
    onClick={() => goToSlide(slide + 1)}
    disabled={slide >= maxSlide}
    className="w-10 h-10 sm:w-11 sm:h-11 rounded-full border border-slate-300 bg-white text-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center hover:border-[#0057B8] hover:text-[#0057B8] disabled:opacity-35 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-colors"
    >
    <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5" />
    </button>
    </div>
    </div>
    </section>
  );
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/ImageWithFallback.tsx`:
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Milk } from 'lucide-react';

interface ImageWithFallbackProps {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  className?: string;
  fallbackTitle?: string;
}

export const ImageWithFallback: React.FC<ImageWithFallbackProps> = ({
  src,
  alt,
  className = '',
  fallbackTitle,
}) => {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setFailed(false);
  }, [src]);

  if (failed || !src) {
    return (
      <div
      className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-100 via-slate-50 to-blue-50/50 text-slate-400 p-4 text-center ${className}`}
      role="img"
      aria-label={alt}
      >
      <Milk className="w-8 h-8 text-[#0057B8]/40 mb-2" strokeWidth={1.5} />
      {fallbackTitle && (
        <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 max-w-[18ch] truncate">
        {fallbackTitle}
        </span>
      )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <img
    src={src}
    alt={alt}
    referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
    loading="lazy"
    onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    className={className}
    />
  );
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/LanguageFlag.tsx`:
```
import React from 'react';
import { Language } from '../types';

interface LanguageFlagProps {
    code: Language;
    className?: string;
}

export const LanguageFlag: React.FC<LanguageFlagProps> = ({
    code,
    className = 'w-5 h-3.5',
}) => {
    const baseClass = `${className} rounded-[2px] overflow-hidden shrink-0 ring-1 ring-black/10 inline-block`;

    switch (code) {
        case 'ru':
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 24 16" className={baseClass} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect width="24" height="5.34" y="0" fill="#FFFFFF" />
                <rect width="24" height="5.34" y="5.33" fill="#0039A6" />
                <rect width="24" height="5.34" y="10.66" fill="#D52B1E" />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'be':
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 24 16" className={baseClass} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect width="24" height="10.67" y="0" fill="#D22730" />
                <rect width="24" height="5.33" y="10.67" fill="#007A33" />
                <rect width="4" height="16" x="0" y="0" fill="#FFFFFF" />
                <path
                d="M1 2h2v2H1V2zm0 4h2v2H1V6zm0 4h2v2H1v-2zm0 4h2v1.5H1V14z"
                fill="#D22730"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'en':
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 24 16" className={baseClass} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect width="24" height="16" fill="#012169" />
                <path d="M0 0L24 16M24 0L0 16" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="3.2" />
                <path d="M0 0L24 16M24 0L0 16" stroke="#C8102E" strokeWidth="1.6" />
                <path d="M12 0V16M0 8H24" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="5" />
                <path d="M12 0V16M0 8H24" stroke="#C8102E" strokeWidth="3" />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'zh':
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 24 16" className={baseClass} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect width="24" height="16" fill="#DE2910" />
                <polygon
                points="4.8,2.2 5.4,4.0 7.3,4.0 5.8,5.1 6.3,6.9 4.8,5.8 3.3,6.9 3.8,5.1 2.3,4.0 4.2,4.0"
                fill="#FFDE00"
                />
                <circle cx="9.2" cy="2.3" r="0.65" fill="#FFDE00" />
                <circle cx="10.5" cy="3.8" r="0.65" fill="#FFDE00" />
                <circle cx="10.5" cy="5.8" r="0.65" fill="#FFDE00" />
                <circle cx="9.2" cy="7.3" r="0.65" fill="#FFDE00" />
                </svg>
            );
    }
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/LanguageSwitcher.tsx`:
```
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { LANGUAGES } from '../data/translations';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { LanguageFlag } from './LanguageFlag';

interface LanguageSwitcherProps {
  compact?: boolean;
  dark?: boolean;
  variant?: 'pills' | 'dropdown';
  direction?: 'down' | 'up';
}

export const LanguageSwitcher: React.FC<LanguageSwitcherProps> = ({
  compact = false,
  dark = false,
  variant = 'pills',
  direction = 'down',
}) => {
  const { language, setLanguage } = useLanguage();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const currentLang = LANGUAGES.find((item) => item.code === language) || LANGUAGES[0];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const handleOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };
    const handleEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false);
    };
      document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleOutside);
      window.addEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);
      return () => {
        document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleOutside);
        window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEsc);
      };
  }, [open]);

  if (variant === 'dropdown') {
    return (
      <div ref={containerRef} className="relative">
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setOpen((prev) => !prev)}
      aria-expanded={open}
      aria-haspopup="listbox"
      aria-label="Select language"
      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-full border text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-150 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-[#0057B8] ${
        dark
        ? 'bg-white/12 hover:bg-white/20 border-white/25 text-white backdrop-blur-sm'
        : 'bg-slate-100/80 hover:bg-slate-200/80 border-slate-200 text-slate-800'
      }`}
      >
      <LanguageFlag code={currentLang.code} className="w-[18px] h-3" />
      <span>{currentLang.label}</span>
      <ChevronDown
      className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 opacity-75 transition-transform duration-150 ${
        open ? 'rotate-180' : ''
      }`}
      />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
        role="listbox"
        aria-label="Language options"
        className={`absolute right-0 w-52 rounded-xl bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-xl border border-slate-200/90 shadow-xl p-1.5 z-50 ${
          direction === 'up' ? 'bottom-full mb-2' : 'top-full mt-2'
        }`}
        >
        {LANGUAGES.map((item) => {
          const active = language === item.code;
          return (
            <button
            key={item.code}
            type="button"
            role="option"
            aria-selected={active}
            onClick={() => {
              setLanguage(item.code);
              setOpen(false);
            }}
            className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-left flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm transition-colors ${
              active
              ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 font-semibold'
              : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
            >
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
            <LanguageFlag code={item.code} className="w-5 h-3.5" />
            <span className="truncate">{item.nativeName}</span>
            <span className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 shrink-0">
            {item.label}
            </span>
            </span>
            {active && <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />}
            </button>
          );
        })}
        </div>
      )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 p-0.5 rounded-lg border transition-colors ${
      dark
      ? 'bg-white/10 border-white/20 backdrop-blur-xs'
      : 'bg-slate-100 border-slate-200/80'
    }`}
    role="group"
    aria-label="Language selector"
    >
    {LANGUAGES.map((item) => {
      const active = language === item.code;
      return (
        <button
        key={item.code}
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setLanguage(item.code)}
        title={item.nativeName}
        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-colors duration-150 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-1 focus-visible:outline-[#0057B8] ${
          active
          ? dark
          ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
          : 'bg-white text-[#0057B8] shadow-xs'
          : dark
          ? 'text-white/80 hover:text-white'
          : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
        }`}
        >
        <LanguageFlag code={item.code} className="w-4 h-3" />
        <span>{compact ? item.label : item.label}</span>
        </button>
      );
    })}
    </div>
  );
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/ProductCard.tsx`:
```
import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { Language, Product } from '../types';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { ImageWithFallback } from './ImageWithFallback';

interface ProductCardProps {
  product: Product;
  variant?: 'default' | 'promo';
}

function getPromoBadge(product: Product, language: Language): string {
  if (product.id === 'mozzarella-pizza') {
    return { ru: 'Хит HoReCa', be: 'Хіт HoReCa', en: 'HoReCa Hit', zh: '餐饮热销' }[language];
  }
  if (product.id === 'goroden-aged') {
    return {
      ru: 'Серия «ГороденЪ»',
      be: 'Серыя «ГорадзенЪ»',
      en: 'Goroden Reserve',
      zh: 'Goroden 臻选',
    }[language];
  }
  if (product.lactoseFree) {
    return {
      ru: '0% лактозы',
      be: '0% лактозы',
      en: 'Lactose-Free',
      zh: '零乳糖',
    }[language];
  }
  if (product.id === 'butter-825') {
    return {
      ru: 'ГОСТ 82,5%',
      be: 'ДАСТ 82,5%',
      en: '82.5% Cream',
      zh: '82.5% 纯脂',
    }[language];
  }
  if ((product.proteinContent ?? 0) >= 8) {
    return {
      ru: 'High Protein',
      be: 'High Protein',
      en: 'High Protein',
      zh: '高蛋白',
    }[language];
  }
  if (product.id === 'milk-classic') {
    return {
      ru: 'Сорт «Экстра»',
      be: 'Гатунак «Экстра»',
      en: 'Extra Grade',
      zh: '特级鲜奶',
    }[language];
  }
  return {
    ru: 'Выбор покупателей',
    be: 'Выбар пакупнікоў',
    en: 'Bestseller',
    zh: '口碑精选',
  }[language];
}

export const ProductCard: React.FC<ProductCardProps> = ({ product, variant = 'default' }) => {
  const { language, localize } = useLanguage();
  const { categories } = useCatalogStore();

  const category = categories.find((c) => c.id === product.category);
  const categoryName = category ? localize(category.name) : product.category;
  const productName = localize(product.name);
  const productDesc = localize(product.description);

  if (variant === 'promo') {
    const promoBadge = getPromoBadge(product, language);
    const selectLabel =
    language === 'ru'
    ? 'Выбрать'
    : language === 'be'
    ? 'Выбраць'
    : language === 'zh'
    ? '查看'
    : 'View';

    return (
      <Link
      to={`/product/${product.slug}`}
      className="group h-full flex flex-col bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-xs hover:border-[#0057B8]/50 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-xl transition-all duration-200 ease-out focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-[#0057B8]"
      >
      <div className="relative aspect-[16/10] w-full bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden border-b border-slate-100 shrink-0">
      <ImageWithFallback
      src={product.image}
      alt={productName}
      fallbackTitle={productName}
      className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.05] transition-transform duration-300 ease-out"
      />

      <div className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 p-2.5 flex items-start justify-between gap-2 pointer-events-none">
      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-[#0057B8]/95 text-white text-[11px] font-semibold shadow-sm backdrop-blur-xs">
      <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
      <span>{promoBadge}</span>
      </span>

      {product.fatContent !== undefined && (
        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-white/95 text-slate-900 border border-slate-200/80 text-[11px] font-bold tabular-nums shadow-xs">
        {product.fatContent}%
        </span>
      )}
      </div>
      </div>

      <div className="p-3.5 sm:p-4 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between gap-2.5">
      <div className="space-y-1.5">
      <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#0057B8]">
      {categoryName}
      </div>

      <h3 className="font-display text-[15px] font-bold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors duration-200 leading-snug line-clamp-2">
      {productName}
      </h3>

      <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">{productDesc}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs tabular-nums">
      <span className="text-slate-500 truncate">
      {product.packages
        .map((p) => p.weight)
        .slice(0, 2)
        .join(' · ')}
        </span>
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] font-semibold group-hover:bg-[#0057B8] group-hover:text-white transition-colors duration-200 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
        <span>{selectLabel}</span>
        <ArrowRight className="w-3 h-3 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-0.5" />
        </span>
        </div>
        </div>
        </Link>
    );
  }

  const packCountLabel =
  language === 'ru'
  ? `${product.packages.length} фас.`
  : language === 'be'
  ? `${product.packages.length} фас.`
  : language === 'zh'
  ? `${product.packages.length} 种包装`
  : `${product.packages.length} packs`;

  const viewLabel =
  language === 'ru'
  ? 'Подробнее'
  : language === 'be'
  ? 'Падрабязней'
  : language === 'zh'
  ? '查看详情'
  : 'Details';

  return (
    <Link
    to={`/product/${product.slug}`}
    className="group h-full flex flex-col bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg transition-all duration-200 ease-out focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-[#0057B8]"
    >
    <div className="aspect-[4/3] w-full bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden border-b border-slate-100 shrink-0">
    <ImageWithFallback
    src={product.image}
    alt={productName}
    fallbackTitle={productName}
    className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.04] transition-transform duration-300 ease-out"
    />
    </div>

    <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:gap-4">
    <div className="space-y-2">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
    <span className="font-medium text-[#0057B8]">{categoryName}</span>
    {product.fatContent !== undefined && (
      <>
      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
      <span>{product.fatContent}%</span>
      </>
    )}
    {product.lactoseFree && (
      <>
      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
      <span>
      {language === 'ru'
        ? 'Без лактозы'
        : language === 'be'
        ? 'Без лактозы'
        : language === 'zh'
        ? '无乳糖'
    : 'Lactose-Free'}
    </span>
    </>
    )}
    </div>

    <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors duration-200 leading-snug">
    {productName}
    </h3>

    <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">{productDesc}</p>
    </div>

    <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
    <span className="truncate">
    {product.packages
      .map((p) => p.weight)
      .slice(0, 3)
      .join(' / ')}
      {product.packages.length > 3 ? ` · ${packCountLabel}` : ''}
      </span>
      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-medium text-slate-700 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors duration-200 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
      {viewLabel}
      <ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:-translate-y-0.5" />
      </span>
      </div>
      </div>
      </Link>
  );
};
```
