```
      const customFeatured = featuredList.filter(
        (p) => !FLAGSHIP_ORDER.includes(p.id) && p.id.startsWith('custom-')
      );
      const prioritized = FLAGSHIP_ORDER.map((id) => featuredList.find((p) => p.id === id)).filter(
        (p): p is Product => Boolean(p)
      );
      const restFeatured = featuredList.filter(
        (p) => !FLAGSHIP_ORDER.includes(p.id) && !p.id.startsWith('custom-')
      );
      return [...customFeatured, ...prioritized, ...restFeatured].slice(0, 4);
    }
    return visibleProducts.filter((p) => p.category === activeCategoryId).slice(0, 4);
  }, [visibleProducts, activeCategoryId]);
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/pages/NewsArticle.tsx`:
```
import React from 'react';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS } from '../data/news';
import { SeoHead } from '../components/SeoHead';
import { ImageWithFallback } from '../components/ImageWithFallback';

export const NewsArticle: React.FC = () => {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const { language, localize, t } = useLanguage();
  const { news } = useCatalogStore();

  const article = news.find((n) => n.slug === id || n.id === id);

  const notFoundCopy = {
    ru: { seoTitle: 'Материал не найден', seoDesc: 'Новость не найдена.', title: 'Публикация не найдена' },
    be: { seoTitle: 'Матэрыял не знойдзены', seoDesc: 'Навіна не знойдзена.', title: 'Публікацыя не знойдзена' },
    en: { seoTitle: 'Article not found', seoDesc: 'Requested article was not found.', title: 'Article not found' },
    zh: { seoTitle: '未找到文章', seoDesc: '未找到相关新闻报道。', title: '未找到该新闻报道' },
  }[language];

  if (!article) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-16 text-center space-y-4">
      <SeoHead title={`${notFoundCopy.seoTitle} — ${t('brandName')}`} description={notFoundCopy.seoDesc} />
      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{notFoundCopy.title}</h1>
      <Link to="/news" className="inline-flex px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg">
      {t('navNews')}
      </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const related = news.filter((n) => n.published && article.relatedIds.includes(n.id));

  return (
    <article className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-12 space-y-8">
    <SeoHead
    title={localize(article.seo.title)}
    description={localize(article.seo.description)}
    canonicalHash={`/news/${article.slug}`}
    />

    <div className="space-y-4">
    <Link to="/news" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0057B8]">
    <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
    <span>{t('navNews')}</span>
    </Link>

    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
    <span className="font-mono">{article.date}</span>
    <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] font-medium">
    {localize(NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS[article.categoryId])}
    </span>
    </div>

    <h1 className="font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">
    {localize(article.title)}
    </h1>

    <p className="text-base sm:text-lg text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{localize(article.excerpt)}</p>
    </div>

    <div className="aspect-[16/9] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
    <ImageWithFallback src={article.image} alt={localize(article.title)} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
    </div>

    <div className="text-base text-slate-800 leading-relaxed space-y-4 max-w-[70ch]">
    <p>{localize(article.content)}</p>
    </div>

    {related.length > 0 && (
      <section className="pt-8 border-t border-slate-200 space-y-4">
      <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
      {language === 'ru'
        ? 'Связанные материалы'
        : language === 'be'
        ? 'Звязаныя матэрыялы'
        : language === 'zh'
        ? '相关新闻'
    : 'Related News'}
    </h2>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
    {related.map((item) => (
      <Link
      key={item.id}
      to={`/news/${item.slug}`}
      className="p-4 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl hover:border-[#0057B8]/50 transition-colors space-y-1.5"
      >
      <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-500 tabular-nums">{item.date}</div>
      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 hover:text-[#0057B8]">{localize(item.title)}</div>
      </Link>
    ))}
    </div>
    </section>
    )}
    </article>
  );
};
```

```
      readMore: '查看详情',
      archiveTitle: '更多新闻报道',
      foundCount: (n: number) => `新闻数: ${n}`,
      emptyTitle: '未找到相关新闻',
      empty: '没有符合当前筛选条件的新闻报道。',
      reset: '重置筛选',
    },
  }[language];

  const publishedSorted = useMemo(
    () => news.filter((n) => n.published).sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date)),
                                  [news]
  );

  const newestArticleId = publishedSorted[0]?.id;

  const years = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<number>(publishedSorted.map((n) => n.year));
    return Array.from(set).sort((a, b) => b - a);
  }, [publishedSorted]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return publishedSorted.filter((n) => {
      if (selectedCategory !== 'all' && n.categoryId !== selectedCategory) return false;
      if (selectedYear !== 'all' && String(n.year) !== selectedYear) return false;
      if (q) {
        const text = [
          n.title.ru,
          n.title.be,
          n.title.en,
          n.title.zh,
          n.excerpt.ru,
          n.excerpt.be,
          n.excerpt.en,
          n.excerpt.zh,
          n.content.ru,
          n.content.be,
          n.content.en,
          n.content.zh,
        ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(' ')
        .toLowerCase();
        if (!text.includes(q)) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [publishedSorted, query, selectedCategory, selectedYear]);

  const hasActiveFilters =
  query.trim().length > 0 || selectedCategory !== 'all' || selectedYear !== 'all';

  const handleReset = () => {
    setQuery('');
    setSelectedCategory('all');
    setSelectedYear('all');
  };

  const featuredArticle = filtered[0];
  const restArticles = filtered.slice(1);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-12 space-y-8">
    <SeoHead
    title={`${t('navNews')} — ${t('brandName')} (milk.by)`}
    description={copy.seoDesc}
    canonicalHash="/news"
    />

    {/* Заголовок и счётчик */}
    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-6">
    <div className="space-y-2.5 max-w-2xl">
    <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#0057B8]">
    {copy.eyebrow}
    </p>
    <h1 className="font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">
    {t('navNews')}
    </h1>
    <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{copy.lead}</p>
    </div>
    <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 tabular-nums shrink-0">
    {copy.foundCount(filtered.length)}
    </div>
    </div>

    {/* Панель поиска и фильтров */}
    <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
    <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3">
    <div className="relative flex-1">
    <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
    <input
    type="text"
    value={query}
    onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
    placeholder={copy.searchPlaceholder}
    className="w-full h-10 pl-10 pr-9 text-base sm:text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#0057B8]/15 transition-all"
    />
    {query && (
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setQuery('')}
      aria-label="Clear search"
      className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-7 h-7 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
      >
      <X className="w-4 h-4" />
      </button>
    )}
    </div>

    <select
    value={selectedYear}
    onChange={(e) => setSelectedYear(e.target.value)}
    aria-label="Filter by year"
    className="h-10 px-3.5 text-base sm:text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8] tabular-nums"
    >
    <option value="all">{copy.allYears}</option>
    {years.map((y) => (
      <option key={y} value={String(y)}>
      {y}
      </option>
    ))}
    </select>
    </div>

    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
    {NEWS_CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
      <button
      key={cat.id}
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(cat.id)}
      className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg border transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
        selectedCategory === cat.id
        ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white border-[#0057B8] shadow-xs'
        : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 hover:text-[#0057B8]'
      }`}
      >
      {localize(cat.label)}
      </button>
    ))}
    </div>

    {hasActiveFilters && (
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleReset}
      className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0057B8] hover:text-[#004494] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
      >
      {copy.reset}
      </button>
    )}
    </div>
    </div>

    {/* Пустое состояние */}
    {filtered.length === 0 ? (
      <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/70 p-12 text-center space-y-3">
      <Newspaper className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-400 mx-auto" strokeWidth={1.5} />
      <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">{copy.emptyTitle}</h2>
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 max-w-md mx-auto">{copy.empty}</p>
      {hasActiveFilters && (
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={handleReset}
        className="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors"
        >
        {copy.reset}
        </button>
      )}
      </div>
    ) : (
      <div className="space-y-10">
      {/* Выделенная последняя / главная новость */}
      {featuredArticle && (
        <Link
        to={`/news/${featuredArticle.slug}`}
        className="group grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-xs hover:border-[#0057B8]/45 hover:shadow-xl transition-all duration-200"
        >
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 relative aspect-[16/10] lg:aspect-auto lg:min-h-[360px] bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
        <ImageWithFallback
        src={featuredArticle.image}
        alt={localize(featuredArticle.title)}
        className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.03] transition-transform duration-300 ease-out"
        />
        {featuredArticle.id === newestArticleId && (
          <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#0057B8] text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-md">
          <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white animate-pulse motion-reduce:animate-none" />
          <span>{copy.latestBadge}</span>
          </div>
        )}
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-5 p-6 sm:p-8 flex flex-col justify-between gap-6 bg-gradient-to-br from-white to-slate-50/60">
        <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5 text-xs tabular-nums">
        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] font-semibold border border-blue-100">
        {localize(NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS[featuredArticle.categoryId])}
        </span>
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-slate-500">
        <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
        {featuredArticle.date}
        </span>
        </div>

        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl sm:text-[1.7rem] font-bold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors leading-snug">
        {localize(featuredArticle.title)}
        </h2>

        <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-600 leading-relaxed line-clamp-4">
        {localize(featuredArticle.excerpt)}
        </p>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200/80 flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#0057B8] group-hover:text-[#004494] transition-colors">
        <span>{copy.readFull}</span>
        <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-1" />
        </span>
        </div>
        </div>
        </Link>
      )}

      {/* Остальные новости */}
      {restArticles.length > 0 && (
        <section className="space-y-5">
        <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
        {copy.archiveTitle}
        </h2>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {restArticles.map((article) => (
          <Link
          key={article.id}
          to={`/news/${article.slug}`}
          className="group bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg transition-all duration-200 ease-out"
          >
          <div className="aspect-[16/10] bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden border-b border-slate-100">
          <ImageWithFallback
          src={article.image}
          alt={localize(article.title)}
          className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.04] transition-transform duration-300 ease-out"
          />
          </div>
          <div className="p-5 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
          <span className="font-mono">{article.date}</span>
          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] font-medium">
          {localize(NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS[article.categoryId])}
          </span>
          </div>
          <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors leading-snug">
          {localize(article.title)}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 line-clamp-3 leading-relaxed">
          {localize(article.excerpt)}
          </p>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
          <span>{copy.readMore}</span>
          <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-1" />
          </div>
          </div>
          </Link>
        ))}
        </div>
        </section>
      )}
      </div>
    )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

```
        {prod.packages.map((p) => p.weight).join(' / ')}
        </div>

        onClick={() => downloadProductB2BSpec(prod, cat, language)}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"

        >
        {copy.quoteBtn}
        </button>
        </div>
        </div>
      );
    })}
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>
    </section>

    <section className="space-y-5">
    <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{copy.stepsTitle}</h2>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5">
    {copy.steps.map((s) => (
      <div
      key={s.num}
      className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-2.5 bg-white hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 transition-colors"

      <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{s.title}</h3>
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{s.desc}</p>
      </div>
    ))}
    </div>
    </section>

    {/* 4. Operational Pillars */}
    <section className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
    {copy.features.map((feat, idx) => {
      const Icon = featureIcons[idx];
      return (
        <div
        key={feat.title}
        className="bg-slate-50/80 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-2.5"
        >
        <Icon className="w-5 h-5 text-[#0057B8]" />
        <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{feat.title}</h3>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{feat.desc}</p>
        </div>
      );
    })}
    </section>

    <B2BInquiryModal
    open={modalOpen}
    onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)}
    product={selectedProduct}
    defaultChannel="wholesale"
    />
    </div>
  );
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/pages/Product.tsx`:
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Download,
  MessageSquare,
  Package,
  Thermometer,
  Clock,
  Layers,
  ShieldCheck,
  Check,
  Globe2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { SeoHead } from '../components/SeoHead';
import { ImageWithFallback } from '../components/ImageWithFallback';
import { ProductGrid } from '../components/ProductGrid';
import { B2BInquiryModal } from '../components/B2BInquiryModal';
import { downloadProductB2BSpec } from '../utils/specExport';

export const Product: React.FC = () => {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const { language, localize, t } = useLanguage();
  const { products, categories } = useCatalogStore();

  const product = products.find((p) => p.slug === id || p.id === id);
  const [selectedPkgId, setSelectedPkgId] = useState<string>('');
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (product && product.packages.length > 0) {
      setSelectedPkgId((prev) =>
      product.packages.some((pkg) => pkg.id === prev) ? prev : product.packages[0].id
      );
    }
  }, [product]);

  if (!product) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-16 text-center space-y-4">
      <SeoHead
      title={`${
        language === 'ru'
        ? 'Товар не найден'
        : language === 'be'
        ? 'Тавар не знойдзены'
        : language === 'zh'
        ? '未找到产品'
        : 'Product not found'
      } — ${t('brandName')}`}
      description={
        language === 'ru'
        ? 'Запрошенный продукт не найден в каталоге.'
        : language === 'be'
        ? 'Запытаны прадукт не знойдзены ў каталогу.'
        : language === 'zh'
        ? '未在目录中找到所请求的产品。'
        : 'Requested product was not found in the catalog.'
      }
      />
      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
      {language === 'ru'
        ? 'Продукт не найден'
        : language === 'be'
        ? 'Прадукт не знойдзены'
        : language === 'zh'
        ? '未找到该产品'
    : 'Product not found'}
    </h1>
    <Link
    to="/catalog"
    className="inline-flex px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors"
    >
    {t('heroPrimaryCta')}
    </Link>
    </div>
    );
  }

  const category = categories.find((c) => c.id === product.category);
  const activePackage =
  product.packages.find((pkg) => pkg.id === selectedPkgId) || product.packages[0];

  const relatedProducts = products.filter(
    (p) => p.visible && product.relatedIds.includes(p.id)
  );

  const gramUnit = language === 'ru' || language === 'be' ? 'г' : language === 'zh' ? '克' : 'g';

  const labels = {
    ru: {
      home: 'Главная',

      qualityLabel: 'Стандарт',

      domesticBadge: 'Розница / РБ',

      related: 'Связанные позиции каталога',
    },
    be: {

      storage: 'Умовы захоўвання',
      packagingType: 'Тып упакоўкі',
      unitsPerBox: 'У тарным месцы',
      specsTitle: 'Характарыстыкі прадукту',
      specsSubtitle: 'Тэхнічныя параметры і склад прадукцыі',
      allPacksTitle: 'Усе фасоўкі і лагістыка каробкі',
      allPacksSubtitle: 'Націсніце на варыянт, каб выбраць яго для запыту КП',
      fat: 'Масавая доля тлушчу',
      fatShort: 'Тлустасць',
      protein: 'Бялок на 100 г',
      proteinShort: 'Бялок / 100 г',

      lactoseShort: 'Прыродная',

      lactoseNaturalVal: '含天然乳糖',
      lactoseFreeShort: '无乳糖',
      lactoseShort: '含天然乳糖',
      qualityLabel: '认证标准',
      exportBadge: '支持出口 B2B',

    },
  }[language];

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-10 space-y-12 sm:space-y-14">
    <SeoHead
    title={localize(product.seo.title)}
    description={localize(product.seo.description)}
    canonicalHash={`/product/${product.slug}`}
    />

    {/* Breadcrumbs */}
    <nav className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
    <Link to="/" className="hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
    {labels.home}
    </Link>
    <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
    <Link to="/catalog" className="hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
    {t('navCatalog')}
    </Link>
    {category && (
      <>
      <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
      <Link to={`/catalog/${category.slug}`} className="hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
      {localize(category.name)}
      </Link>
      </>
    )}
    <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
    <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium">{localize(product.name)}</span>
    </nav>

    {/* Hero Product Overview: Anchored Image Left + Pack Selector & CTAs Right */}
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 lg:gap-10 items-start">
    {/* Left Column: Static Image + Key Nutrition/Compliance Metrics */}
    <div className="lg:col-span-6 space-y-4">
    <div className="aspect-[4/3] w-full rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 shadow-xs">
    <ImageWithFallback
    src={product.image}
    alt={localize(product.name)}
    fallbackTitle={localize(product.name)}
    className="w-full h-full object-cover"
    />
    </div>

    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5 tabular-nums">
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-3.5 py-2.5">
    <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{labels.fatShort}</div>
    <div className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5">
    {product.fatContent !== undefined ? `${product.fatContent}%` : '—'}
    </div>
    </div>
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-3.5 py-2.5">

    {product.proteinContent !== undefined
      ? `${product.proteinContent} ${gramUnit}`
      : '—'}
      </div>
      </div>
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-3.5 py-2.5">
      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{labels.lactose}</div>

      {product.lactoseFree ? labels.lactoseFreeShort : labels.lactoseShort}
      </div>
      </div>
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-3.5 py-2.5">
      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{labels.qualityLabel}</div>
      <div className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-[#0057B8] mt-0.5 truncate">
      FSSC 22000
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

        <Link
        to={`/catalog/${category.slug}`}

        >
        {localize(category.name)}
        </Link>
      )}
      {product.exportReady ? (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-medium border border-emerald-200/70">

        {labels.exportBadge}
        </span>
      ) : (
        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 font-medium">
        {labels.domesticBadge}
        </span>
      )}
      {product.lactoseFree && (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 font-medium border border-amber-200/70">
        <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        {labels.lactoseFreeShort}
        </span>
      )}
      </div>

      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight leading-tight">
      {localize(product.seo.h1) || localize(product.name)}
      </h1>

      <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
      {localize(product.description)}
      </p>
      </div>

      {/* Multi-Packaging Variant Selector */}
      {product.packages.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-3 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700">
        {labels.packagesTitle}
        </span>
        <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
        {product.packages.length} SKU
        </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {product.packages.map((pkg) => {
          const isSelected = activePackage?.id === pkg.id;
          return (
            <button
            key={pkg.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSelectedPkgId(pkg.id)}
            aria-pressed={isSelected}
            className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl border transition-all duration-150 tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap ${
              isSelected
              ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white border-[#0057B8] shadow-xs'
              : 'bg-white text-slate-800 border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/50 hover:text-[#0057B8]'
            }`}
            >
            {pkg.weight}
            </button>
          );
        })}
        </div>

        {activePackage && (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5 bg-slate-50/90 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 text-sm tabular-nums">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <Package className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{labels.weight}</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{activePackage.weight}</div>
          </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{labels.shelfLife}</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
          {localize(activePackage.shelfLife)}
          </div>
          </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <Thermometer className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{labels.storage}</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
          {localize(activePackage.storage)}
          </div>
          </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{labels.unitsPerBox}</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900 break-all">
          {activePackage.unitsPerBox} {labels.boxUnitsShort} · GTIN {activePackage.gtin}
          </div>
          </div>
          </div>
          <div className="sm:col-span-2 pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-200/80 text-xs text-slate-600">
          <span className="text-slate-500">{labels.packagingType}: </span>
          <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">
          {localize(activePackage.packagingType)}
          </span>
          </div>
          </div>
        )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* B2B Actions */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row flex-wrap gap-3 pt-1">
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setModalOpen(true)}
      className="w-full sm:w-auto inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-xl hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors shadow-xs"
      >
      <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
      <span>{t('requestSalesBtn')}</span>
      </button>
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => downloadProductB2BSpec(product, category, language)}
      className="w-full sm:w-auto inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
      >
      <Download className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
      <span>{t('downloadSpecBtn')}</span>
      </button>
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      {/* Dedicated Full-Width Section: Technical Specifications & All Pack Formats */}
      <div className="pt-8 border-t border-slate-200 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start">
      {/* Dynamic Category-Specific Characteristics Table */}
      <section className="lg:col-span-6 space-y-4">
      <div>
      <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
      {labels.specsTitle}
      </h2>
      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">{labels.specsSubtitle}</p>
      </div>

      <dl className="divide-y divide-slate-200 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden text-sm tabular-nums bg-white">
      {product.fatContent !== undefined && (
        <div className="px-4 py-3.5 flex justify-between gap-4 bg-white">
        <dt className="text-slate-500">{labels.fat}</dt>
        <dd className="font-semibold text-slate-900 min-w-0 break-words text-right">
        {product.fatContent}%
        </dd>
        </div>
      )}
      {product.proteinContent !== undefined && (
        <div className="px-4 py-3.5 flex justify-between gap-4 bg-slate-50/60">
        <dt className="text-slate-500">{labels.protein}</dt>
        <dd className="font-semibold text-slate-900 min-w-0 break-words text-right">
        {product.proteinContent} {gramUnit}
        </dd>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="px-4 py-3.5 flex justify-between gap-4 bg-white">
      <dt className="text-slate-500">{labels.lactose}</dt>
      <dd className="font-semibold text-slate-900 min-w-0 break-words text-right">
      {product.lactoseFree ? labels.lactoseFreeVal : labels.lactoseNaturalVal}
      </dd>
      </div>

      {Object.entries(product.characteristics).map(([key, value], idx) => {
        const schemaItem = category?.characteristicSchema.find((f) => f.key === key);
        const label = schemaItem ? localize(schemaItem.label) : key;
        return (
          <div
          key={key}
          className={`px-4 py-3.5 flex justify-between gap-4 ${
            idx % 2 === 0 ? 'bg-slate-50/60' : 'bg-white'
          }`}
          >
          <dt className="text-slate-500">{label}</dt>
          <dd className="font-semibold text-slate-900 min-w-0 break-words text-right">
          {localize(value)}
          </dd>
          </div>
        );
      })}
      </dl>
      </section>

      {/* Interactive All-Packs Logistics Matrix */}
      {product.packages.length > 0 && (
        <section className="lg:col-span-6 space-y-4">
        <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
        {labels.allPacksTitle}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">{labels.allPacksSubtitle}</p>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2.5">
        {product.packages.map((pkg) => {
          const isSelected = activePackage?.id === pkg.id;
          return (
            <button
            key={pkg.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSelectedPkgId(pkg.id)}
            className={`w-full text-left rounded-2xl border p-4 transition-all duration-150 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 tabular-nums ${
              isSelected
              ? 'bg-blue-50/70 border-[#0057B8] shadow-xs ring-1 ring-[#0057B8]/20'
              : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 hover:bg-slate-50/60'
            }`}
            >
            <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
            {pkg.weight}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-medium">
            {localize(pkg.shelfLife)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            {localize(pkg.storage)}
            </span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 truncate">
            {localize(pkg.packagingType)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400">
            {pkg.unitsPerBox} {labels.boxUnitsShort} · GTIN {pkg.gtin}
            </div>
            </div>

            <div
            className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-colors ${
              isSelected
              ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white'
              : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400'
            }`}
            >
            <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
        </div>
        </section>
      )}
      </div>

      {/* Related Products */}
      {relatedProducts.length > 0 && (
        <section className="space-y-6 pt-8 border-t border-slate-200">
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
        {labels.related}
        </h2>
        <ProductGrid products={relatedProducts} />
        </section>
      )}

      <B2BInquiryModal
      open={modalOpen}
      onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)}
      product={product}
      selectedPackageId={activePackage?.id}
      defaultChannel={product.exportReady ? 'export' : 'wholesale'}
      />
      </div>
  );
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/pages/SitemapPage.tsx`:

```
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Download, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

import { SeoHead } from '../components/SeoHead';

  const { products, categories, news, redirects } = useCatalogStore();

    ru: {

      colNote: 'Примечание',
    },
    be: {
      seoDesc: 'Аўтаматычна абнаўляемы sitemap.xml, hreflang-сувязі 4 моўных версій і табліца 301-рэдырэктаў са старых URL milk.by.',
      h1: 'XML-карта сайта, SEO-аўдыт і 301-рэдырэкты',
      lead: 'Аўтаматычная генерацыя ЧПУ-структуры з падтрымкай hreflang (RU / BE / EN / ZH) і адсутнасцю дубляў.',
      downloadXml: 'Спампаваць sitemap.xml',
      uniqTitle: 'Унікальнасць SEO Title',

      redirectsCount: 'Наладжана 301-рэдырэктаў',
      rulesUnit: 'правілаў',
      redirectsHeading: 'Карта 301-рэдырэктаў са старых URL milk.by',
      colOld: 'Стары URL (milk.by)',
      colCode: 'Код',
      colNew: 'Новы кананічны URL',
      colNote: 'Заўвага',
    },
    en: {
      seoDesc: 'Auto-generated sitemap.xml, 4-language hreflang links, and 301 redirect rules from legacy milk.by URLs.',
      h1: 'XML Sitemap, SEO Audit & 301 Redirects',
      lead: 'Auto-generated clean URL sitemap with 4-language hreflang links and legacy 301 redirects.',
      downloadXml: 'Download sitemap.xml',
      uniqTitle: 'Unique SEO Titles',
      noDupes: '(No duplicates)',
      langVersions: 'Language Versions (hreflang)',
      redirectsCount: 'Configured 301 Redirects',
      rulesUnit: 'rules',
      redirectsHeading: 'Legacy URL 301 Redirect Mapping',
      colOld: 'Legacy URL (milk.by)',
      colCode: 'Code',
      colNew: 'Canonical Target URL',
      colNote: 'Note',
    },
    zh: {
      seoDesc: '自动更新的 sitemap.xml、四种语言 hreflang 关联及 milk.by 旧版链接 301 重定向映射表。',
      h1: 'XML 站点地图、SEO 审计与 301 重定向',
      lead: '自动生成支持四种语言 hreflang (RU / BE / EN / ZH) 且无重复标题的规范化 URL 站点地图。',
      downloadXml: '下载 sitemap.xml',
      uniqTitle: 'SEO 标题唯一性',
      noDupes: '（无重复）',
      langVersions: '语言版本 (hreflang)',
      redirectsCount: '已配置 301 重定向',
      rulesUnit: '条规则',
      redirectsHeading: 'milk.by 旧版链接 301 重定向映射表',
      colOld: '旧版 URL (milk.by)',
      colCode: '状态码',
      colNew: '新版规范 URL',
      colNote: '说明',
    },
  }[language];

  const xmlContent = useMemo(
    () => generateXmlSitemap(products, categories, news),
                             [products, categories, news]
  );

  const seoAudit = useMemo(() => {
    const visibleProducts = products.filter((p) => p.visible);
    const titles = visibleProducts.map((p) => p.seo.title[language] || p.seo.title.ru);
    const uniqueTitles = new Set(titles);
    return {
      totalProducts: visibleProducts.length,
      uniqueTitlesCount: uniqueTitles.size,
      noDuplicates: titles.length === uniqueTitles.size,
    };
  }, [products, language]);

  const handleDownloadXml = () => {
    const blob = new Blob([xmlContent], { type: 'application/xml;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'sitemap.xml';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-10 space-y-10">
    <SeoHead
    title={`XML Sitemap & SEO Audit — ${t('brandName')}`}
    description={copy.seoDesc}
    canonicalHash="/sitemap"
    />

    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-5">
    <div>
    <h1 className="font-display text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">{copy.h1}</h1>
    <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-1">{copy.lead}</p>
    </div>

    <button
    type="button"
    onClick={handleDownloadXml}
    className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 self-start sm:self-auto"
    >
    <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
    <span>{copy.downloadXml}</span>
    </button>
    </div>

    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 tabular-nums">
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 bg-white">
    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{copy.uniqTitle}</div>
    <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
    <span>
    {seoAudit.uniqueTitlesCount} / {seoAudit.totalProducts}{' '}
    {seoAudit.noDuplicates ? copy.noDupes : ''}
    </span>
    </div>
    </div>
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 bg-white">
    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{copy.langVersions}</div>
    <div className="mt-1 font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
    4 (RU · BE · EN · ZH)
    </div>
    </div>
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 bg-white">
    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{copy.redirectsCount}</div>
    <div className="mt-1 font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
    {redirects.length} {copy.rulesUnit}
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>

    <section className="space-y-3">
    <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
    {copy.redirectsHeading}
    </h2>
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-x-auto bg-white">
    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
    <thead>
    <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 font-semibold text-slate-600">
    <th className="py-3 px-4">{copy.colOld}</th>
    <th className="py-3 px-4">{copy.colCode}</th>
    <th className="py-3 px-4">{copy.colNew}</th>
    <th className="py-3 px-4">{copy.colNote}</th>
    </tr>
    </thead>
    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
    {redirects.map((rule) => {
      const targetPath = rule.to.replace(/^\/?#/, '') || '/';
    return (
      <tr key={rule.from}>
      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono text-slate-600">{rule.from}</td>
      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono font-semibold text-[#0057B8]">{rule.code}</td>
      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono">
      <Link to={targetPath} className="text-[#0057B8] hover:underline">
      {rule.to}
      </Link>
      </td>
      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-600">{rule.note}</td>
      </tr>
    );
    })}
    </tbody>
    </table>
    </div>
    </section>

    <section className="space-y-3">
    <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
    sitemap.xml (Live Preview)
    </h2>
    <pre className="p-4 bg-slate-950 text-slate-100 rounded-xl overflow-x-auto text-xs font-mono leading-relaxed max-h-[420px]">
    {xmlContent}
    </pre>
    </section>
    </div>
  );
};
```

```
export type Language = 'ru' | 'be' | 'en' | 'zh';

export type LocalizedText = Record<Language, string>;

export interface PackageVariant {
  id: string;
  weight: string;
  weightGrams: number;
  shelfLife: LocalizedText;
  storage: LocalizedText;
  packagingType: LocalizedText;
  unitsPerBox: number;
  gtin: string;
}

export interface ProductSeo {
  title: LocalizedText;
  description: LocalizedText;
  h1: LocalizedText;
}

export interface Product {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  category: string;
  subcategory: string;
  name: LocalizedText;
  description: LocalizedText;
  image: string;
  fatContent?: number;
  proteinContent?: number;
  lactoseFree?: boolean;
  exportReady?: boolean;
  featured?: boolean;
  visible: boolean;
  packages: PackageVariant[];
  characteristics: Record<string, LocalizedText>;
  useCases: string[];
  relatedIds: string[];
  seo: ProductSeo;
}

export interface CategorySubcategory {
  id: string;
  name: LocalizedText;
}

export interface CharacteristicField {
  key: string;
  label: LocalizedText;
}

export interface Category {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: LocalizedText;
  description: LocalizedText;
  subcategories: CategorySubcategory[];
  filterKeys: ('fat' | 'lactoseFree' | 'highProtein' | 'exportReady')[];
  characteristicSchema: CharacteristicField[];
  seo: {
    title: LocalizedText;
    description: LocalizedText;
  };
}

export type NewsCategoryId = 'company' | 'export' | 'products' | 'awards';

export interface NewsArticle {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  date: string;
  year: number;
  categoryId: NewsCategoryId;
  title: LocalizedText;
  excerpt: LocalizedText;
  content: LocalizedText;
  image: string;
  published: boolean;
  relatedIds: string[];
  seo: {
    title: LocalizedText;
    description: LocalizedText;
  };
}

export interface RedirectRule {
  from: string;
  to: string;
  code: 301;
  note: string;
}

export interface B2BInquiry {
  company: string;
  contactName: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  country: string;
  productId?: string;
  packageId?: string;
  volume: string;
  message: string;
  channel: 'export' | 'wholesale' | 'general';
}
```

```
const SYNONYM_GROUPS: string[][] = [
  ['моцарелл', 'моцарел', 'мацарэл', 'mozzarell', 'mozarell'],
['городен', 'горадзен', 'goroden', 'пармезан', 'выдержан'],
['беллавист', 'белавіст', 'bellavist', 'бри', 'brie', 'камамбер'],
['молок', 'малак', 'milk'],
['сом', 'сам', 'smp', 'порошок', 'powder', 'сухое'],
['йогурт', 'егурт', 'yogurt', 'yoghurt'],
['творог', 'тварог', 'творож', 'твараж', 'tvorog', 'cottage'],
['сырок', 'сырк', 'глазир', 'глазур', 'curd'],
['масл', 'butter'],
['сливк', 'сливок', 'вяршк', 'cream'],
['сметан', 'смятан', 'sour'],
['кефир', 'кефір', 'кефер', 'kefir'],
['морожен', 'марозі', 'пломбир', 'пламбір', 'plombir', 'ice'],
['сыворот', 'сыроват', 'whey'],
['белок', 'белк', 'бялок', 'бялк', 'протеин', 'protein'],
['лактоз', 'lactose'],
['пицц', 'піц', 'pizza'],
['выпечк', 'кандытар', 'кондитер', 'pastry', 'baking'],
['экспорт', 'экспарт', 'export', 'horeca', 'опт', 'b2b'],
];

function normalizeText(input: string): string {
  return input
  .toLowerCase()
  .replace(/ё/g, 'е')
  .replace(/(\d),(\d)/g, '$1.$2')
  .replace(/[«»"'`()[\]]/g, ' ')
  .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  .trim();
}

function decodeKeyboardLayout(raw: string): string | null {
  const trimmed = raw.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!trimmed || !/^[a-z[\];',.\s]+$/.test(trimmed)) return null;
  // Don't convert normal English dairy words
  if (/(milk|cheese|pizza|yogurt|butter|cream|kefir|ice|wine|protein|lactose|export|powder|sugar|baking|coffee|brie|goroden|mozzarella)/i.test(trimmed)) {
    return null;
  }
  const converted = trimmed
  .split('')
  .map((ch) => EN_TO_RU_MAP[ch] ?? ch)
  .join('');
  // Must look plausible in Russian (contains vowels)
  if (!/[аеиоуыэюя]/.test(converted)) return null;
  return converted;
}

function stemToken(token: string): string {
  if (token.length <= 3) return token;
  let t = token.replace(/ё/g, 'е');
  // Special fleeting vowel normalizations
  if (/^бело?к/.test(t)) return 'белк';
  if (/^сливо?к/.test(t)) return 'сливк';
  if (/^сыро?к/.test(t)) return 'сырк';

  if (/[а-яіў]/.test(t) && t.length >= 5) {
    const stripped = t.replace(
      /(ами|ями|ого|его|ому|ему|ыми|ими|ах|ях|ов|ев|ей|ом|ем|ую|юю|ое|ее|ые|ие|ой|ий|ый|ая|яя|ы|и|а|я|у|ю|о|е|ь)$/,
                               ''
    );
    if (stripped.length >= 3) t = stripped;
  } else if (/^[a-z]+$/.test(t) && t.length >= 5) {
    const stripped = t.replace(/(ies|es|s|ing|ed)$/, '');
    if (stripped.length >= 4) t = stripped;
  }
  return t;
}

function levenshteinBounded(a: string, b: string, maxDist: number): number {
  if (Math.abs(a.length - b.length) > maxDist) return maxDist + 1;
  if (a === b) return 0;
  const lenA = a.length;
  const lenB = b.length;
  let prev = Array.from({ length: lenB + 1 }, (_, j) => j);

  for (let i = 1; i <= lenA; i++) {
    const curr = [i];
    let rowMin = curr[0];
    for (let j = 1; j <= lenB; j++) {
      const cost = a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1;
      const val = Math.min(prev[j] + 1, curr[j - 1] + 1, prev[j - 1] + cost);
      curr.push(val);
      if (val < rowMin) rowMin = val;
    }
    if (rowMin > maxDist) return maxDist + 1;
    prev = curr;
  }
  return prev[lenB];
}

function expandSynonyms(stem: string): string[] {
  const expanded = new Set<string>([stem]);
  for (const group of SYNONYM_GROUPS) {
    if (group.some((alias) => stem.startsWith(alias) || alias.startsWith(stem))) {
      for (const alias of group) expanded.add(alias);
    }
  }
  return Array.from(expanded);
}

function extractTokens(normQuery: string): string[] {
  const tokens = new Set<string>();
  const words = normQuery.match(/[a-zа-яіў0-9%.-]+/gi) || [];
  for (const raw of words) {
    const w = raw.replace(/^[.-]+|[.-]+$/g, '');
    if (!w || w.length < 2) continue;
    if (STOPWORDS.has(w)) continue;
    tokens.add(w);
  }

  const cjkRuns = normQuery.match(/[\u4e00-\u9fff]+/g) || [];
  for (const run of cjkRuns) {
    if (run.length <= 2) {
      tokens.add(run);
    } else {
      tokens.add(run);
      for (let i = 0; i <= run.length - 2; i++) {
        tokens.add(run.slice(i, i + 2));
      }
    }
  }
  return Array.from(tokens);
}

function parseShelfLifeDays(text: string): number {
  const nums = text.match(/\d+/g);
  if (!nums || nums.length === 0) return 0;
  const val = parseInt(nums[0], 10);
  if (!Number.isFinite(val)) return 0;
  if (/месяц|month|月/i.test(text)) return val * 30;
  return val;
}

interface ParsedQuery {
  normQuery: string;
  tokens: string[];
  stems: string[];
  intents: Set<IntentKey>;
  categoryBoosts: Map<string, string>;
  fatMax?: number;
  fatMin?: number;
  fatExact?: number;
  proteinMin?: number;
  targetWeightGrams?: number;
  preferBulk?: boolean;
  preferRetail?: boolean;
}

function parseUserQuery(rawQuery: string, language: Language): ParsedQuery {
  const normQuery = normalizeText(rawQuery);
  const intents = new Set<IntentKey>();
  const categoryBoosts = new Map<string, string>();

  if (/(пицц|pizza|піц|披萨|плав|запек|растяг|тягуч|пиццери)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('pizza');
  }
  if (/(без\s*лактоз|безлактоз|lactose|непереносим|零乳糖|无乳糖|нет\s*лактоз)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('lactoseFree');
  }
  if (/(белок|белк|бялок|бялк|протеин|protein|спорт|фитнес|мышц|пп\b|高蛋白|蛋白)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('highProtein');
  }
  if (/(без\s*сахар|без\s*цукр|no\s*sugar|sugar\s*free|zero\s*sugar|无糖|零蔗糖)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('noSugar');
  }
  if (/(вин\p{L}*|він\p{L}*|wine|дегустац|сырн\p{L}*\s+тарел|葡萄酒|红酒|品鉴)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('wine');
  }
  if (/(выпеч|кондитер|кандытар|торт|чизкейк|тирамису|взбиван|ўзбіван|крем|ганаш|pastry|bak|whipping|круассан|烘焙|甜点)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('baking');
  }
  if (/(кофе|капучино|латте|бариста|coffee|latte|barista|咖啡)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('coffee');
  }
  if (/(экспорт|экспарт|опт|аптов|horeca|export|b2b|мешк|мяшк|монолит|gacc|китай|ази|контейнер|出口|大宗)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('export');
  }
  if (/(длительн|долго|без\s*холодильник|uht|ультрапастериз|180\s*сут|24\s*мес|long\s*shelf|ambient|常温|长保)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('longShelf');
  }
  if (/(десерт|сладк|перекус|ребен|детям|dessert|sweet|snack|甜品)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('dessert');
  }
  if (/(низк\p{L}*\s+жир|маложир|обезжир|легк|лёгк|диет|похуд|low\s*fat|skim|低脂)/iu.test(normQuery)) {
    intents.add('lowFat');
  }

  for (const catRule of CATEGORY_KEYWORDS) {
    if (catRule.pattern.test(normQuery)) {
      categoryBoosts.set(catRule.categoryId, catRule.labels[language]);
    }
  }

  // Numeric fat extraction
  let fatMax: number | undefined;
  let fatMin: number | undefined;
  let fatExact: number | undefined;

  const fatMaxMatch = normQuery.match(/(?:до|меньше|ниже|max|under|below|<=?)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*%/i);
  if (fatMaxMatch) {
    fatMax = parseFloat(fatMaxMatch[1]);
  } else if (intents.has('lowFat')) {
    fatMax = 5;
  }

  const fatMinMatch = normQuery.match(/(?:от|более|выше|min|over|above|>=?)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*%/i);
  if (fatMinMatch) {
    fatMin = parseFloat(fatMinMatch[1]);
  }

  if (fatMax === undefined && fatMin === undefined) {
    const exactFatMatch = normQuery.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*%|\b(82\.5|72\.5|3\.2|2\.5|1\.5|0\.1)\b/);
    if (exactFatMatch) {
      fatExact = parseFloat(exactFatMatch[1] || exactFatMatch[2]);
    }
  }

  // Numeric protein extraction
  let proteinMin: number | undefined;
  const proteinMatch = normQuery.match(/(?:бел\p{L}*|protein)\s*(?:от|более|>=?|min)?\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)|(?:от|>=?)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:г|g)?\s*(?:бел\p{L}*|protein)/iu);
  if (proteinMatch) {
    const val = parseFloat(proteinMatch[1] || proteinMatch[2]);
    if (Number.isFinite(val) && val > 0) proteinMin = val;
  }

  // Target package weight extraction
  let targetWeightGrams: number | undefined;
  const weightMatch = normQuery.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(кг|kg|г|g|мл|ml|л|l)(?:\b|$)/i);
  if (weightMatch) {
    const num = parseFloat(weightMatch[1]);
    const unit = weightMatch[2].toLowerCase();
    if (Number.isFinite(num) && num > 0) {
      targetWeightGrams = unit === 'кг' || unit === 'kg' || unit === 'л' || unit === 'l'
      ? Math.round(num * 1000)
      : Math.round(num);
    }
  }

  const preferBulk = /(horeca|опт|мешк|монолит|больш\p{L}*\s+упаков|2\.5\s*кг|20\s*кг|25\s*кг|1\s*кг|bulk)/iu.test(normQuery);
  const preferRetail = /(порцион|маленьк|в\s*стакан|розниц|retail|small)/iu.test(normQuery);

  const tokens = extractTokens(normQuery);
  const stems = Array.from(new Set(tokens.map(stemToken)));

  return {
    normQuery,
    tokens,
    stems,
    intents,
    categoryBoosts,
    fatMax,
    fatMin,
    fatExact,
    proteinMin,
    targetWeightGrams,
    preferBulk,
    preferRetail,
  };
}

function pickRecommendedPackage(product: Product, parsed: ParsedQuery): PackageVariant | undefined {
  if (product.packages.length === 0) return undefined;
  if (parsed.targetWeightGrams !== undefined) {
    let best = product.packages[0];
    let minDiff = Math.abs(best.weightGrams - parsed.targetWeightGrams);
    for (const pkg of product.packages) {
      const diff = Math.abs(pkg.weightGrams - parsed.targetWeightGrams);
      if (diff < minDiff) {
        minDiff = diff;
        best = pkg;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }
  if (parsed.intents.has('pizza') || parsed.intents.has('export') || parsed.preferBulk) {
    return [...product.packages].sort((a, b) => b.weightGrams - a.weightGrams)[0];
  }
  if (parsed.intents.has('longShelf')) {
    return [...product.packages].sort(
      (a, b) =>
      parseShelfLifeDays(b.shelfLife.ru || b.shelfLife.en || '') -
      parseShelfLifeDays(a.shelfLife.ru || a.shelfLife.en || '')
    )[0];
  }
  return product.packages[0];
}

function evaluateQuery(
  rawQuery: string,
  products: Product[],
  categories: Category[],
  language: Language
): { results: SmartMatchResult[]; criteria: SmartCriterion[] } {
  const parsed = parseUserQuery(rawQuery, language);
  const visibleProducts = products.filter((p) => p.visible);
  const catMap = new Map(categories.map((c) => [c.id, c]));
  const gUnit = language === 'ru' || language === 'be' ? 'г' : language === 'zh' ? '克' : 'g';

  const criteria: SmartCriterion[] = [];
  for (const [catId, label] of parsed.categoryBoosts.entries()) {
    criteria.push({ id: `cat-${catId}`, label });
  }
  for (const intent of parsed.intents) {
    criteria.push({ id: `intent-${intent}`, label: INTENT_CRITERIA_LABELS[intent][language] });
  }
  if (parsed.fatMax !== undefined) {
    criteria.push({
      id: 'fat-max',
      label:
      language === 'ru'
    ? `Жирность ≤ ${parsed.fatMax}%`
    : language === 'be'
    ? `Тлустасць ≤ ${parsed.fatMax}%`
    : language === 'zh'
    ? `脂肪 ≤ ${parsed.fatMax}%`
    : `Fat ≤ ${parsed.fatMax}%`,
    });
  }
  if (parsed.fatMin !== undefined) {
    criteria.push({
      id: 'fat-min',
      label:
      language === 'ru'
    ? `Жирность ≥ ${parsed.fatMin}%`
    : language === 'be'
    ? `Тлустасць ≥ ${parsed.fatMin}%`
    : language === 'zh'
    ? `脂肪 ≥ ${parsed.fatMin}%`
    : `Fat ≥ ${parsed.fatMin}%`,
    });
  }
  if (parsed.proteinMin !== undefined) {
    criteria.push({
      id: 'prot-min',
      label:
      language === 'ru'
    ? `Белок ≥ ${parsed.proteinMin} г`
    : language === 'be'
    ? `Бялок ≥ ${parsed.proteinMin} г`
    : language === 'zh'
    ? `蛋白 ≥ ${parsed.proteinMin} 克`
    : `Protein ≥ ${parsed.proteinMin}g`,
    });
  }
  if (parsed.targetWeightGrams !== undefined) {
    const label =
    parsed.targetWeightGrams >= 1000
    ? `${parsed.targetWeightGrams / 1000} ${language === 'ru' || language === 'be' ? 'кг' : 'kg'}`
    : `${parsed.targetWeightGrams} ${gUnit}`;
    criteria.push({ id: 'weight', label });
  }

  const scored: SmartMatchResult[] = [];

  for (const product of visibleProducts) {
    let score = 0;
    const reasons: string[] = [];

    const nameAll = normalizeText(
      [product.name.ru, product.name.be, product.name.en, product.name.zh].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
    );
    const currentName = normalizeText(product.name[language] || product.name.ru || '');
    const cat = catMap.get(product.category);
    const catNameAll = normalizeText(
      cat ? [cat.name.ru, cat.name.be, cat.name.en, cat.name.zh].filter(Boolean).join(' ') : product.category
    );
    const descAll = normalizeText(
      [product.description.ru, product.description.be, product.description.en, product.description.zh]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(' ')
    );
    const charAll = normalizeText(
      Object.values(product.characteristics)
      .flatMap((c) => [c.ru, c.be, c.en, c.zh])
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(' ')
    );
    const packAll = normalizeText(
      product.packages
      .map((p) => `${p.weight} ${p.packagingType.ru} ${p.packagingType.en} ${p.shelfLife.ru}`)
      .join(' ')
    );

    const nameWords = extractTokens(nameAll);
    const catWords = extractTokens(catNameAll);

    // 1. Exact / prefix / phrase match on Product Name (highest priority!)
    if (currentName === parsed.normQuery || nameAll === parsed.normQuery) {
      score += 160;
    } else if (currentName.startsWith(parsed.normQuery)) {
      score += 130;
    } else if (parsed.normQuery.length >= 2 && nameAll.includes(parsed.normQuery)) {
      score += 95;
    }

    // 2. Category boost from detected category intent
    if (parsed.categoryBoosts.has(product.category)) {
      score += 80;
    }

    // 3. Semantic Intent matching
    if (parsed.intents.has('pizza') && product.useCases.includes('pizza')) {
      score += product.id === 'mozzarella-pizza' ? 85 : 50;
      reasons.push(INTENT_REASONS.pizza[language]);
    }
    if (parsed.intents.has('lactoseFree') && (product.lactoseFree || product.useCases.includes('lactose-free'))) {
      score += product.id === 'milk-lactose-free' ? 90 : 70;
      reasons.push(INTENT_REASONS.lactoseFree[language]);
    }
    if (
      parsed.intents.has('highProtein') &&
      ((product.proteinContent ?? 0) >= 8 || product.useCases.includes('high-protein'))
    ) {
      score += (product.proteinContent ?? 0) >= 15 ? 70 : 55;
      if (product.id === 'greek-yogurt-protein' || product.id === 'tvorog-grain') score += 15;
      reasons.push(`${INTENT_REASONS.highProtein[language]} (${product.proteinContent ?? 0} ${gUnit} / 100${gUnit})`);
    }
    if (parsed.intents.has('noSugar') && (product.id === 'greek-yogurt-protein' || product.id === 'milk-lactose-free' || product.category === 'cheese' || product.category === 'milk' || product.category === 'tvorog')) {
      score += product.id === 'greek-yogurt-protein' ? 60 : 25;
      if (product.id === 'greek-yogurt-protein') reasons.push(INTENT_REASONS.noSugar[language]);
    }
    if (parsed.intents.has('wine') && product.useCases.includes('wine')) {
      score += 80;
      reasons.push(INTENT_REASONS.wine[language]);
    }
    if (parsed.intents.has('baking') && product.useCases.includes('baking')) {
      score += 65;
      reasons.push(INTENT_REASONS.baking[language]);
    }
    if (
      parsed.intents.has('coffee') &&
      (product.useCases.includes('coffee') || product.id === 'cream-33' || product.id === 'milk-classic')
    ) {
      score += product.useCases.includes('coffee') || product.id === 'cream-33' ? 70 : 45;
      reasons.push(INTENT_REASONS.coffee[language]);
    }
    if (parsed.intents.has('export') && product.exportReady) {
      score += product.category === 'dry-dairy' || product.id === 'butter-825' ? 60 : 40;
      reasons.push(INTENT_REASONS.export[language]);
    }
    if (parsed.intents.has('longShelf')) {
      const maxDays = Math.max(
        0,
        ...product.packages.map((p) => parseShelfLifeDays(p.shelfLife.ru || p.shelfLife.en || ''))
      );
      if (maxDays >= 90) {
        score += 65;
        reasons.push(INTENT_REASONS.longShelf[language]);
      }
    }
    if (parsed.intents.has('dessert') && product.useCases.includes('dessert')) {
      score += 65;
      reasons.push(INTENT_REASONS.dessert[language]);
    }

    // 4. Numeric constraints
    if (parsed.fatMax !== undefined) {
      if ((product.fatContent ?? 99) <= parsed.fatMax) {
        score += 55;
        reasons.push(`${INTENT_REASONS.lowFat[language]} (${product.fatContent}%)`);
      } else if (parsed.intents.size === 0 && parsed.categoryBoosts.size === 0) {
        score -= 40;
      }
    }
    if (parsed.fatMin !== undefined) {
      if ((product.fatContent ?? 0) >= parsed.fatMin) {
        score += 55;
      } else {
        score -= 40;
      }
    }
    if (parsed.fatExact !== undefined && product.fatContent !== undefined) {
      if (Math.abs(product.fatContent - parsed.fatExact) < 0.05) {
        score += 85;
      }
    }
    if (parsed.proteinMin !== undefined) {
      if ((product.proteinContent ?? 0) >= parsed.proteinMin) {
        score += 60;
        reasons.push(`${INTENT_REASONS.highProtein[language]} (${product.proteinContent} ${gUnit})`);
      } else {
        score -= 45;
      }
    }
    if (parsed.targetWeightGrams !== undefined) {
      const hasClosePack = product.packages.some(
        (pkg) => Math.abs(pkg.weightGrams - parsed.targetWeightGrams!) <= parsed.targetWeightGrams! * 0.15
      );
      if (hasClosePack) score += 50;
    }

    // 5. Token, Stem, Synonym & Typo matching across weighted fields
    let nameStemHits = 0;
    let catStemHits = 0;
    let packHits = 0;
    let charHits = 0;
    let descHits = 0;

    for (const rawTok of parsed.tokens) {
      const stem = stemToken(rawTok);
      const aliases = expandSynonyms(stem);

      const matchesAny = (haystack: string) => aliases.some((a) => haystack.includes(a));

      if (matchesAny(nameAll)) {
        nameStemHits++;
        // Bonus if word in product name literally starts with rawTok or stem
        if (nameWords.some((nw) => nw.startsWith(stem) || nw.startsWith(rawTok))) {
          score += 20;
        }
      } else if (rawTok.length >= 4) {
        // Bounded Levenshtein fuzzy match on product name words
        const maxDist = rawTok.length >= 7 ? 2 : 1;
        if (nameWords.some((nw) => levenshteinBounded(rawTok, nw, maxDist) <= maxDist)) {
          score += 38;
        }
      }

      if (matchesAny(catNameAll)) {
        catStemHits++;
      } else if (rawTok.length >= 4) {
        const maxDist = rawTok.length >= 7 ? 2 : 1;
        if (catWords.some((cw) => levenshteinBounded(rawTok, cw, maxDist) <= maxDist)) {
          score += 30;
        }
      }

      if (matchesAny(packAll)) packHits++;
      if (matchesAny(charAll)) charHits++;
      if (matchesAny(descAll)) descHits++;
    }

    score += nameStemHits * 55;
    score += catStemHits * 45;
    score += packHits * 25;
    score += charHits * 10;
    score += descHits * 8;

    // Multi-token completeness bonus
    if (parsed.tokens.length >= 2 && nameStemHits + catStemHits >= parsed.tokens.length) {
      score += 35;
    }

    if (score > 0) {
      if (reasons.length === 0) {
        if (nameStemHits > 0 || catStemHits > 0) {
          reasons.push(
            language === 'ru'
            ? 'Точное совпадение по названию и категории'
            : language === 'be'
            ? 'Дакладнае супадзенне па назве і катэгорыі'
            : language === 'zh'
            ? '匹配产品名称与分类'
            : 'Matches product name and category'
          );
        } else {
          reasons.push(
            language === 'ru'
            ? 'Подходит по характеристикам и составу'
            : language === 'be'
            ? 'Падыходзіць па характарыстыках і складзе'
            : language === 'zh'
            ? '符合产品特性与规格'
            : 'Matches specifications and packaging'
          );
        }
      }

      scored.push({
        product,
        score,
        reasons,
        recommendedPackage: pickRecommendedPackage(product, parsed),
      });
    }
  }

  scored.sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
    return Number(Boolean(b.product.featured)) - Number(Boolean(a.product.featured));
  });

  // Suppress weak incidental description-only hits when strong matches exist (fixes "молоко" matching cheese ingredients)
  const topScore = scored[0]?.score ?? 0;
  const filtered =
  topScore >= 85
  ? scored.filter((item) => item.score >= 30)
  : scored.filter((item) => item.score >= 15);

  return { results: filtered, criteria };
}

export function runSmartSearch(
  query: string,
  products: Product[],
  categories: Category[],
  language: Language
): SmartSearchResponse {
  const trimmed = query.trim();
  if (!trimmed) {
    return { results: [], criteria: [] };
  }

  const primary = evaluateQuery(trimmed, products, categories, language);
  if (primary.results.length > 0) {
    return primary;
  }

  // Try automatic wrong-keyboard-layout recovery (e.g. "vjkjrj" -> "молоко")
  const layoutFixed = decodeKeyboardLayout(trimmed);
  if (layoutFixed) {
    const recovered = evaluateQuery(layoutFixed, products, categories, language);
    if (recovered.results.length > 0) {
      return {
        results: recovered.results,
        correctedQuery: layoutFixed,
        criteria: [
          {
            id: 'layout-fix',
            label:
            language === 'ru'
            ? `Исправлена раскладка: «${layoutFixed}»`
            : language === 'be'
            ? `Выпраўлена раскладка: «${layoutFixed}»`
            : language === 'zh'
            ? `已自动纠正输入: "${layoutFixed}"`
            : `Auto-corrected: "${layoutFixed}"`,
          },
          ...recovered.criteria,
        ],
      };
    }
  }

  return primary;
}

export function runSmartProductMatch(
  query: string,
  products: Product[],
  language: Language,
  categories: Category[] = []
): SmartMatchResult[] {
  return runSmartSearch(query, products, categories, language).results.slice(0, 6);
}
```

```
import { Category, Language, NewsArticle, Product } from '../types';

export function downloadProductB2BSpec(
  product: Product,
  category: Category | undefined,
  language: Language
): void {
  const name = product.name[language] || product.name.ru;
  const desc = product.description[language] || product.description.ru;
  const catName = category ? category.name[language] || category.name.ru : product.category;

  const lines: string[] = [
    '============================================================',
    'OAO "MOLOCHNY MIR" (milk.by) — B2B / EXPORT PRODUCT SPEC SHEET',
    '28 Gaspadarchaya St, Grodno 230005, Republic of Belarus',
    'Quality Systems: FSSC 22000 | ISO 9001 | HACCP',
    '============================================================',
    '',
    `PRODUCT: ${name}`,
    `CATEGORY: ${catName}`,
    `PRODUCT ID / SLUG: ${product.id} (/${product.slug})`,
    `FAT CONTENT: ${product.fatContent !== undefined ? `${product.fatContent}%` : 'N/A'}`,
    `PROTEIN CONTENT: ${product.proteinContent !== undefined ? `${product.proteinContent} g / 100g` : 'N/A'}`,
    `LACTOSE FREE: ${product.lactoseFree ? 'Yes (<0.01%)' : 'No (Natural dairy lactose)'}`,
    `EXPORT READY: ${product.exportReady ? 'Yes (Certified)' : 'Domestic / Regional'}`,
    '',
    'DESCRIPTION:',
    desc,
    '',
    'TECHNICAL CHARACTERISTICS:',
    ...Object.entries(product.characteristics).map(([key, val]) => {
      const schemaField = category?.characteristicSchema.find((f) => f.key === key);
      const label = schemaField ? schemaField.label[language] || schemaField.label.ru : key;
      return `  - ${label}: ${val[language] || val.ru}`;
    }),
    '',
    'AVAILABLE PACKAGING & LOGISTICS VARIANTS:',
    ...product.packages.map(
      (pkg, idx) =>
        `  ${idx + 1}. Weight: ${pkg.weight} | Shelf Life: ${pkg.shelfLife[language] || pkg.shelfLife.ru} | Storage: ${
          pkg.storage[language] || pkg.storage.ru
        } | Packaging: ${pkg.packagingType[language] || pkg.packagingType.ru} | Units/Box: ${
          pkg.unitsPerBox
        } | GTIN: ${pkg.gtin}`
    ),
    '',
    'EXPORT & WHOLESALE CONTACT:',
    '  Export Dept: ved@milk.by | +375 (152) 60-07-52',
    '  Domestic Sales: sales@milk.by | +375 (152) 45-39-42',
    `  Generated: ${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`,
  ];

  const blob = new Blob([lines.join('\n')], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `molochny-mir-${product.slug}-spec-${language}.txt`;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

export function generateXmlSitemap(
  products: Product[],
  categories: Category[],
  news: NewsArticle[],
  baseUrl = 'https://milk.by'
): string {
  const langs: Language[] = ['ru', 'be', 'en', 'zh'];
  const staticPaths = ['', '/catalog', '/export', '/partners', '/news', '/about', '/contacts'];

  const urls: string[] = [];

  for (const path of staticPaths) {
    urls.push(
      `  <url>\n    <loc>${baseUrl}/#${path || '/'}</loc>\n    <changefreq>weekly</changefreq>\n    <priority>${
        path === '' ? '1.0' : '0.8'
      }</priority>\n    ${langs
        .map((l) => `<xhtml:link rel="alternate" hreflang="${l}" href="${baseUrl}/#/${l}${path}" />`)
        .join('\n    ')}\n  </url>`
    );
  }

  for (const cat of categories) {
    urls.push(
      `  <url>\n    <loc>${baseUrl}/#/catalog/${cat.slug}</loc>\n    <changefreq>weekly</changefreq>\n    <priority>0.85</priority>\n  </url>`
    );
  }

  for (const prod of products.filter((p) => p.visible)) {
    urls.push(
      `  <url>\n    <loc>${baseUrl}/#/product/${prod.slug}</loc>\n    <changefreq>weekly</changefreq>\n    <priority>0.9</priority>\n  </url>`
    );
  }

  for (const item of news.filter((n) => n.published)) {
    urls.push(
      `  <url>\n    <loc>${baseUrl}/#/news/${item.slug}</loc>\n    <lastmod>${item.date}</lastmod>\n    <changefreq>monthly</changefreq>\n    <priority>0.7</priority>\n  </url>`
    );
  }

  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9"\n        xmlns:xhtml="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml">\n${urls.join(
    '\n'
  )}\n</urlset>`;
}
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/App.tsx`:
```
/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { HashRouter, Routes, Route, Link, useLocation, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { LanguageProvider, useLanguage } from './hooks/useLanguage';
import { CatalogStoreProvider } from './hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { Header } from './components/Header';
import { Footer } from './components/Footer';
import { Home } from './pages/Home';
import { Catalog } from './pages/Catalog';
import { Category } from './pages/Category';
import { Product } from './pages/Product';
import { News } from './pages/News';
import { NewsArticle } from './pages/NewsArticle';
import { Export } from './pages/Export';
import { Partners } from './pages/Partners';
import { About } from './pages/About';
import { Contacts } from './pages/Contacts';
import { Admin } from './pages/Admin';
import { SitemapPage } from './pages/SitemapPage';
import { Language } from './types';

const SUPPORTED_LANGS: Language[] = ['ru', 'be', 'en', 'zh'];

const ScrollToTop: React.FC = () => {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }, [pathname]);
  return null;
};

const NotFound: React.FC = () => {
  const { language } = useLanguage();

  const copy = {
    ru: {
      title: '404 — Страница не найдена',
      desc: 'Запрошенный адрес не существует. Воспользуйтесь каталогом продукции или вернитесь на главную.',
      home: 'На главную',
      catalog: 'Каталог продукции',
    },
    be: {
      title: '404 — Старонка не знойдзена',
      desc: 'Запытаны адрас не існуе. Скарыстайцеся каталогам прадукцыі або вярніцеся на галоўную.',
      home: 'На галоўную',
      catalog: 'Каталог прадукцыі',
    },
    en: {
      title: '404 — Page Not Found',
      desc: 'The requested page does not exist. Browse our dairy catalog or return to the homepage.',
      home: 'Homepage',
      catalog: 'Product Catalog',
    },
    zh: {
      title: '404 — 页面未找到',
      desc: '您访问的地址不存在。请浏览产品目录或返回首页。',
      home: '返回首页',
      catalog: '产品目录',
    },
  }[language];

  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center space-y-4">
    <h1 className="font-display text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">{copy.title}</h1>
    <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">{copy.desc}</p>
    <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
    <Link
    to="/"
    className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494]"
    >
    {copy.home}
    </Link>
    <Link
    to="/catalog"
    className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200"
    >
    {copy.catalog}
    </Link>
    </div>
    </div>
  );
};

const LanguageScopedRoutes: React.FC = () => {
  const { lang } = useParams<{ lang: string }>();
  const { setLanguage } = useLanguage();
  const lastSyncedParam = useRef<string | undefined>(undefined);

  const isSupported = Boolean(lang && SUPPORTED_LANGS.includes(lang as Language));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSupported && lang && lastSyncedParam.current !== lang) {
      lastSyncedParam.current = lang;
      setLanguage(lang as Language);
    }
  }, [isSupported, lang, setLanguage]);

  if (!isSupported) {
    return <NotFound />;
  }

  return (
    <Routes>
    <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
    <Route path="catalog" element={<Catalog />} />
    <Route path="catalog/:category" element={<Category />} />
    <Route path="product/:id" element={<Product />} />
    <Route path="news" element={<News />} />
    <Route path="news/:id" element={<NewsArticle />} />
    <Route path="export" element={<Export />} />
    <Route path="partners" element={<Partners />} />
    <Route path="about" element={<About />} />
    <Route path="contacts" element={<Contacts />} />
    <Route path="admin" element={<Admin />} />
    <Route path="sitemap" element={<SitemapPage />} />
    <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
    </Routes>
  );
};

export default function App() {
  return (
    <LanguageProvider>
    <CatalogStoreProvider>
    <HashRouter>
    <ScrollToTop />
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-white text-slate-900">
    <Header />
    <main className="flex-1 min-w-0">
    <Routes>
    <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
    <Route path="/catalog" element={<Catalog />} />
    <Route path="/catalog/:category" element={<Category />} />
    <Route path="/product/:id" element={<Product />} />
    <Route path="/news" element={<News />} />
    <Route path="/news/:id" element={<NewsArticle />} />
    <Route path="/export" element={<Export />} />
    <Route path="/partners" element={<Partners />} />
    <Route path="/about" element={<About />} />
    <Route path="/contacts" element={<Contacts />} />
    <Route path="/admin" element={<Admin />} />
    <Route path="/sitemap" element={<SitemapPage />} />
    <Route path="/:lang/*" element={<LanguageScopedRoutes />} />
    <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
    </Routes>
    </main>
    <Footer />
    </div>
    </HashRouter>
    </CatalogStoreProvider>
    </LanguageProvider>
  );
}
```
